# 生成、共同实现与伦理：完整理论链对照表 / Generativity, co-realization and ethics: the connected theory

内容更新 / Content updated: **2026-11-07** · Asia/Bangkok · **94 条导航项 / 94 indexed entries**

[总成果目录 / Results catalogue](README.md) · [全部章节 / All chapters](ARGUMENTS-A-L.md) · [后半章节 / More chapters](ARGUMENTS-M-Z.md) · [Lean 声明 / Lean declarations](LEAN-DECLARATIONS.md)

本表覆盖一条相连的理论链：生成根基、共同实现、生生伦理、情境判断、学习、良知与智慧，以及相关证明、评审和来源。它按论证关系整理，完整列出这条链的主要命题与支撑入口；不把整个仓库的其他理论分支或每个辅助引理逐行混入。精细章节与声明可由总成果目录继续查阅。各条目保留其时的标签，[08 §10](../chapters/08-METHOD.md#retired-labels) 一次说明。该处 G0 对应存活之意义（`LivingSignificance`），即 Lean `G0` 的今名；条目文字中的 G0 也指它所述者，即整体中的生及继续生成的正面意义，曾取作起点，所说更多。存活之意义在延续条件之下得出；G0 所述多于它者不是现论的前提：活的任一读法都不含继续生发；生生作为方向之所向，以扩大相比较；C，即扩大可形成者更好，给出次序，一般而言只有它（自我关切无需 C 即可给一种情形排序），延续条件与 C 都不断言（[07 §3](../chapters/07-SIGNIFICANCE.md#condition)、[07 §4](../chapters/07-SIGNIFICANCE.md#scope-and-order)）。J1–J4 是实践承诺，P 编号是其时的工作板条目。本理论的核心成果（08 §8 所列）都不是新数学：每项近于定义，或是已知成果的重述；这一判断出自一次 AI 审查，其检索并不穷尽（[08 §8](../chapters/08-METHOD.md#known-results)）。请求某一步的原话保存在所链来源中，只作来源，不作任何一步的根据。条目给出的论证是工作在条目所述范围内的理由，未必是这一步当时所以走的原因。条目标明尚无论证之处，写“未给论证”或“应请求，未给论证”；既未论证也未标明的步骤，尚未为此读过。

This table covers one connected chain: generative grounding, joint realization, Shengsheng ethics, situated judgment, learning, Liangzhi and wisdom, together with proofs, reviews and sources. It organizes the chain’s main propositions and supporting entries by their argumentative roles; it does not mix in every other repository branch or every auxiliary lemma. The wider catalogue provides the finer chapter and declaration inventory. The entries keep the labels of their time; [08 §10](../chapters/08-METHOD.md#retired-labels) explains them once. There G0 maps to living significance (`LivingSignificance`), the present name of the Lean `G0`; in the entries' prose G0 also names what it stated, the favorable significance of whole life and further generation, once taken as a starting point, which says more. Living significance follows under the continuation condition; what G0 stated beyond it is no premise of the current account: no reading of living includes further generation; 生生, the object of the direction, is compared by widening; and C, widening what can form is better, gives order (self-concern ranks one case with no C), neither the continuation condition nor C asserted ([07 §3](../chapters/07-SIGNIFICANCE.md#condition), [07 §4](../chapters/07-SIGNIFICANCE.md#scope-and-order)). J1–J4 are the practical commitments, and P-numbers name the board items of their time. None of the theory's central results, as 08 §8 ranks them, is new mathematics: each is near-definitional or a known result restated, by an AI review whose search was not exhaustive ([08 §8](../chapters/08-METHOD.md#known-results)). Words that asked for a step are kept in the linked sources as provenance and warrant no step. An argument an entry gives is the work's reason at the scope the entry states; it need not be why the step was taken at the time. Where an entry marks a step as resting on none yet, it says "with no argument given" or "on request, with no argument given"; a step it neither argues nor marks has not yet been read for this.

## 核心论断 / The central claim

> 新形成的关系使原来不能共同发生的生活与进一步形成条件能够共同实现时，整体获得了新的实现方式；其正面意义可以就在这种共同形成中，而不必先变成发起者的收益。

> When newly formed relations enable forms of living and conditions for further formation that could not previously occur together to be jointly realized, the whole acquires a new mode of realization; its favorable significance may lie in this co-formation itself, without first becoming a return to its initiator.

这段话是 AI 撰写的候选论证及其英文对译；共同形成的某些机制与表示已得到检查，其普遍伦理识别仍在论证。 / This is an AI-authored candidate argument and its English translation. Some mechanisms and representations of joint formation are checked; its universal ethical identification remains under investigation.

[所引论证与完整范围 / Quoted argument and full scope](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/ZERO-AXIOM-CLASSIFICATION-INTEGRATION.md#4-正面伦理推论得到更准确的对象但仍需要根据)

## 主链与读法 / Chain and reading guide

同一生成展开 → 组织及后续条件 → 有支持的共同实现 → 整体质量与真实得失 → 情境理由 → 学习、关怀、良知与智慧 → 实践及校准。这是阅读主线，不是已完成的单向推导；组织—调节—学习—关切的形成路径与规范研究相互回接。各连接的根据和完成程度分列于表中。

One unfolding → organization and further conditions → supported co-realization → whole quality and real gains/losses → situated reasons → learning, concern, Liangzhi and wisdom → practice and calibration. This is a reading guide, not a completed one-way derivation; organization–regulation–learning–concern and normative inquiry reconnect with one another. The tables distinguish each connection’s warrant and completion scope.

表中的理论、方法、机制、命题与检查包是不同维护单元，并非同等地位的独立理论。‘已检查’仅及于所列定义、前提、对应与历史版本；‘候选’和‘未完成’保留其真实研究状态。

Theories, methods, mechanisms, propositions and verification packages are different maintenance units, not equally established independent theories. Checked results retain their definitions, hypotheses, correspondence and source versions; candidates and open questions remain identified.

## 一、共同根基与正面论断 / Shared grounding and the positive claim

| 编号 / ID | 理论或命题 / Theory or proposition | 中文论断 | English formulation | 范围与状态 / Scope and status | 原文、证明与评审 / Sources, proofs and reviews |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C01 | 变化整体与其中的生成形态<br>Becoming as a whole and its generative forms | 根基必然性的对象是Becoming整体；生成性是其manifold中的部分。 在形成之中（生成与退化皆然），活动形成并转化后续活动的条件；组织、认识、行动与评价在同一展开中分化，并通过实际相连的机制及其对应得到说明。 系统通过实际关系辨认，边界须保留相关依存；预测充分性与构成参与分别说明。 包容生成与退化的整体范围须保留接续相容性；情境可以通过同一活动实际改变，而不从每一步任意重选。 作用构成不预设每个原生接口均有确定因果次序。 局部损失可经更广关系的形成产生；恢复须保留其实际使用的关系及可用条件。 | The foundational necessity target is Becoming as a whole; generativity is within its manifold. Within formation, generative and degenerative alike, activity forms and transforms conditions of further activity; organization, knowing, action and evaluation differentiate within one unfolding and are explained through connected mechanisms and their correspondence. Systems are identified through actual relations; boundaries retain relevant dependencies, with predictive sufficiency distinguished from constitutive participation. An inclusive scope covering generation and degeneration must retain continuation coherence; context may actually change through the same activity without being freely reselected at each step. Operative constitution does not presume a definite causal order at every native interface. Local loss can arise through the formation of wider relations; restoration requires the relations and access that the claimed recovery uses. | 相连说明在条件范围内已完成；无假设目标继续。既有11项有限重演定理之外，持续阶段25项新定理、27项声明审计；构成可证源对应并传递至复合项，实际适用、逻辑／识别根据与普遍范围仍待证成。 直接构成论证保留已辨认关系的必要内容，排除同一架构普遍实现的额外门槛；全目标域的归属与覆盖尚未证成。本轮无新定理。 后续系统研究新增27项定理／41项空依赖声明审计，另18项继承审计：联合可接纳域中两个充分区域可以没有最小共同支持。最小性限可判定子区域，无最小支持结论不限。形成域内的有限词可行性已证；实际构成及更强普遍根据仍开放。 变化根据接续新增19项定理／21项空审计，59项继承声明原样复查：由形成与后续路径导出精确可达域及有限接续对应。发生、当前能力、普遍约束与精确范围各需相应覆盖；本地域输入已消除，实际普遍无假设根据仍未完成。 本轮一般论证澄清：实际参与及“可以发生”已有根据；相容共同实现具有一般构成约束力。必要构成条件尚不独自提供形成、转化及可能性变化的普遍充分说明。无新定理或公理；不要求所有具体应用无误，较强目标仍未完成。 内在分化路线的原文检验进一步保留实际作用与比较关系，拒绝将共同呈现直接升级为普遍必然自我展开；无新公理或定理，作者反审不冒充独立验收。 随后设定的标准（每项根基主张须演绎必然）出于对一次澄清提问的回答，未给论证：它说的是根基目标所要求者，不是这一目标何以应作此要求；既有实际支持保留，但不独自完成两个“可以”根基。新演绎检验5项定理／7项空审计刻画一般的实际到必然推论条件；比较关系的真实适用与普遍过程法则仍待奠基。 整体消前提接续有五项实查定理（含健全性构造重证）／七项新空审计，继承17项审计为8空、9项propext。由有良基证明族可构造空基础推导；尚无实际过程法则的实例，不将证明依赖当作现实的分离层级。 缘起路线检验区分相依构成与模态范围；不补设独立生成整体或每个随机结果的决定理由。必要的普遍条件命题可作根基，但适用条件不能暗含待证规律。无新定理，较强演绎目标未完成。 包容历史的相容性新增20项Lean定理／30项声明审计及28项继承审计：合并步骤精确保留原历史并集，当且仅当该并集对接合封闭。保留情境给出精确实现；后继情境支持从同一历史导出，实际改变与终止均保留。57项依赖报告空，终止证明含标准propext。实际关系与解释仍给定，强的零实质公理基础未完成。 直接闭合尝试未成功：缺少的是共同作用关系的普遍演绎根据，不是把全部具体规律必然化。无新增定理或公理。 量子过程主文献比较区分联合操作结构与次序选项清单、时间实现与原生因果次序。此次修订开放根基候选，不新增量子证明、经验结果或零公理完成；既有有限历史证明保留范围。 后续诊断修正保留实际形成与调用的作用内容；静态构型遗漏这些内容不反驳完整解释，普遍必然性仍待证明。 明示实振幅控制计算新增27项Lean定理／54项审计（44空、10标准，无最终经典选择）。分支权重保持而固定响应改变；约化状态的统一逆失败，保留共同交互却能精确反转。量子操作及解释仍为输入；不声称因果不可分离或普遍零公理证明。 退化、消散与持存都在同一展开之内，所以生成性不是每个生成／变化都须满足的条件；撤回将普遍生成性作为Becoming完成门槛的做法；此次纠正演绎对象，不新增公理或整体完成证明。 修正后的整体演绎区分包容范围、关系构成与具体作用形态；此前普遍生成性缺口不设定其完成门槛。从包容到构成的直接论证仍未完成：自 10 月 22 日起，它作为读法，对从一次交换读出的世界前提已得论证，在那里中心规律对从交换读出者成立（central-laws-over-every-exchange）；每个生成／变化都有这样的交换、以及局部性的根据，仍然开放；黑格尔原典区分最初范畴与原因、条件。无新增定理。<br>Connected articulation complete at conditional scope; assumption-free grounding continues. Beyond 11 earlier finite-replay theorems, the sustained investigation has 25 new theorems and 27 audits. Construction can prove source links and propagate correspondence; actual applicability, logical/recognitional warrant and universal scope remain open. The direct constitutive argument preserves established relational content and rejects universal use of one architecture as an extra gate; full-scope attribution and coverage remain unestablished. No new theorem in this assessment. The subsequent systems study adds 27 theorems / 41 empty declaration audits plus 18 inherited audits: joint admissibility can allow two sufficient regions with no least support. Minimality is among decidable subregions; absence of a least support is unrestricted. Formed-domain finite-word feasibility is proved; actual constitution and stronger universal warrant remain open. The variation-warrant continuation adds 19 theorems / 21 empty audits, retaining 59 inherited audits: formation histories derive the exact attained domain and finite continuation correspondence. Occurrence, current capability, universal constraints and exact ranges need different coverage. The independent local domain input is removed; actual universal assumption-free warrant remains unfinished. The general argument now credits actual participation and permissive existence, and earns compatible joint realization as a general constitutive constraint. Necessary realization alone does not supply the common sufficient explanation of formation, transformation and changing possibilities. No new theorem or axiom; infallibility of every application is not required, and the stronger target remains unfinished. A primary-source test of immanent differentiation preserves actual operation while distinguishing comparison from causal scope; shared presentation does not alone establish universal necessary self-development. No new axiom or theorem; author counterreview is not independent acceptance. The standard then set, that every foundational claim be deductively necessary, was set in an answer to a clarifying question, with no argument given: it says what the foundation's target asks, not why it should ask it. Actual support remains, but does not alone complete the two permissive commitments. Five new theorems / seven empty audits characterize uniform actuality-to-necessity transfer; actual comparison scope and the universal process law remain ungrounded at the required strength. Whole-theory discharge adds five checked theorems, including a constructive soundness reproof, and seven empty new audits; 17 inherited reports retain eight empty and nine propext dependencies. A well-founded proof family yields empty-basis derivations, but no certificate for the actual process law is supplied. Proof dependency is not an ontological division of the whole. The dependent-origination test distinguishes relational constitution from modal coverage, without adding an independent generative substance or determination of each stochastic outcome. Necessary universal conditionals remain eligible, but their antecedents must not smuggle in the target law. No new theorem; the stronger deductive goal remains unfinished. Scope correction: foundational necessity does not require deducing every exact local possibility range from the foundation alone. The common formation-and-change law and foundational capability claims still require deductive warrant. Conditions of organizational identity do not exclude actual transformation or ending. No new theorem or compiler run. The temporal test articulates retained efficacy through formed present relations and dispositions without requiring an independent record. Historical belonging alone does not prove universal operative inheritance. A primary Bergson comparison adds no universal preservation, irreversibility or novelty premise and no theorem; the stronger goal remains unfinished. Subsequent original-proof repair removes propext from soundness and eight descendants. The three core/tree proof modules now have 24 empty audits; the affected 32-module build has 423 audits, 313 empty and 110 standard. Statements, definitions and local premises are unchanged. Old receipts retain historical scope; the substantive law remains unproved. Inquiry repair preserves all fifteen original statements and operational definitions, removes thirteen standard dependency lists and adds one helper reproof. The six-module logical/inquiry closure has 63 empty audits; the 32-module build has 424 reports, 327 empty and 97 standard. Concrete local basis certificates are now axiom-free in the reported sense; the actual universal law remains unproved. This self-grounding continuation stops at a wall: enacted challenge, intelligibility and immanent understanding have not supplied the common generative law and scope. No new theorem or universal impossibility claim; other research retains its scope. Formation compilation repair preserves the law and premises: all 35 native definitions have empty reports, and eleven existing reports lose their dependencies. The current 32-module build has 424 reports: 338 empty, 86 standard. Seven old/new equivalence controls are separate; six retain legacy dependencies. The actual universal foundation remains unproved. Relational formation adds 20 Lean theorems / 31 empty audits: arbitrary independent transitions and their finite sequences preserve a recombinable domain, excluding that entire explanation class for a newly restricted target. Faithful separate recoding preserves the criterion. Inheritance, selection, common modes and erasure remain controls; actual modal scope and universal grounding are unproved. The resumed study derives finite-history continuation updates and the exact boundary of unchanged profiles: 18 new theorems and 25 inherited audits are empty. Sequential restrictions or newly enabled capabilities can force history-dependent possibilities. Substantive admissibility, operative interpretation and universal representational reach remain to be grounded; assumption-free completion is not claimed. Aspect warrant adds 19 theorems / 25 empty audits, with 49 inherited empty audits: an invertible mixing preserves complete declared dynamics but changes coordinate factorization; transporting original reading meanings preserves the joint-domain test. The formation theorem is unchanged. Actual role constitution and the universal process law remain ungrounded at the deductive standard above. The next continuation checks an omission in the word profile against the unchanged native formation: correlation preserves all finite feasible words while changing a later outcome, alongside the inherited exclusion of independent factorization for the fixed readings. Nine new theorems and 78 inherited/selected native audits are empty. Existing endpoint-aware composition repairs the abstraction; local inputs and representational omissions are not themselves foundational failures. Actual operative roles and the common law remain to be grounded. Operation-relative distinctions add 28 theorems / 37 empty audits, with 74 inherited audits: general successor comparisons are covariant, and locally idempotent operations construct fixed representatives characterizing invariant readings. Native conditions, non-idempotent and action-word controls remain explicit. This reduces a separate descriptive-split input without discharging actual operation or universal-law warrant. Return conditions add 23 theorems / 28 new empty audits, with 87 inherited/selected audits: two native return questions identify the four configurations, while the stated modal interface cannot define the formed relation. Adding a count exactly preserves original histories and endpoint questions but eliminates nonempty full-state returns; projection recovers the native relation. Identity scope, actual identification and universal deductive warrant remain distinct obligations. A direct explanation-to-law audit adds four small theorems / four empty audits: uniform discharge of the witness condition requires an admitted witness, while target coverage and the substantive law connection remain explicit. No universal explainability axiom is added; the common law remains deductively ungrounded. Formation-symmetry tests add 22 theorems / 29 empty audits, plus 18 inherited audits: symmetric initial conditions and symmetry-preserving laws retain symmetry at a unique endpoint. Branching admits retained differences; seeded refinement moves asymmetry into the start and fails uniform lifting. Neither determinism nor physical randomness is added to the universal foundation. The constitutive-powers assessment credits operative content within relations; adding a fitting name to each process does not alone establish a universal restriction. It reuses the checked total-extension theorem with original receipt verified, adding no axiom or theorem. The common actual law remains deductively ungrounded. The finite-basis interpretation argument is now checked in the original calculus: full pointwise closed-theory coverage gives one common admitted interpretation, including the empty basis. Separate-row matching is insufficient; local premises remain. Ten new theorems / 12 new empty audits plus 12 inherited audits do not establish actual admissibility or root necessity. The individuation assessment develops the affirmative account through formed relations and roles, reusing the established method history. Locating formative capacity within the whole does not alone prove universal necessity. The primary text’s commitments are not adopted as axioms; no new theorem or build is counted. 2026-10-09 local-law continuation: transitions are constructed from admitted histories and their summary; finite continuation exactness holds iff same-code histories have identical future profiles, without a separate updater or endpoint-lifting premise. Six new theorems and five definitions have 11 empty new audits plus 43 empty inherited audits. The actual history language and its warrant remain supplied; the stronger foundation is unfinished. Inclusive-history coherence adds 20 Lean theorems / 30 new declaration audits plus 28 inherited audits: pooling steps preserves exactly the original history union iff that union is closed under joining. Retained context gives an exact realization; successor context support is derived from the same history, preserving actual change and termination. 57 dependency reports are empty; terminality uses standard propext. Actual relation and interpretation remain supplied; the strong zero-substantive-axiom foundation is unfinished. The direct closure attempt remains unsuccessful: the missing deduction concerns the universal operative relationship, not necessity of every particular law. No further theorem or postulate is added. A primary quantum-process comparison distinguishes joint operational structure from an inventory of order alternatives, and temporal implementation from native causal order. This revises the open foundation candidate; it supplies no new quantum proof, empirical result or zero-axiom completion. Existing finite-trace proofs retain their scope. The subsequent diagnostic correction preserves actual formation and use: their omission from a static arrangement does not refute the fuller explanation; universal necessity remains unproved. A declared real-amplitude control calculation adds 27 Lean theorems / 54 audits (44 empty; 10 standard, no final classical choice). Branch weights remain equal while the fixed response changes; a reduced-state uniform inverse fails, but retained joint interaction reverses exactly. Quantum operations and interpretation remain inputs; no causal-nonseparability or universal zero-axiom proof is claimed. Degeneration, dissolution and persistence lie within the same unfolding, so generativity is not a condition every becoming must satisfy; universal generativity is withdrawn as a completion gate for Becoming. This is a correction of the deduction’s target, not a new axiom or completed whole-theory proof. The corrected whole deduction separates inclusive scope, relational constitution and particular operative modes. Earlier universal-generativity gap reports do not set its completion gate. The direct inclusion-to-constitution argument remains incomplete: since 22 October it is argued, as a reading, for the World premises read off one exchange, where the central laws hold of what is read off it (central-laws-over-every-exchange); that every becoming has such an exchange, and the warrant of locality, stay open; primary Hegel passages distinguish the opening category from cause or condition. | C03 | 生生不息的正面方向<br>Shengsheng as a positive direction | 整体创造更多生、更丰富的可能与多样性，并使复杂组织继续发展的能力，是正在论证的善的方向。 | The whole’s formation of more life, richer possibilities and diversity, and capacities for further organizational development is the proposed favorable direction. | 2026-10-07 在一次回答中作为方向提出，未给论证；论题 T8 现今在两条前提之下论证它，其根据仍开放（07 §8）；G0 曾为起点，今不是现论的前提：存活之意义（每一个活的主张从每一个立场看都有意义），即今名之下的 Lean `G0`（08 §10），在延续条件之下得出（理论不断言，07 §3）；G0 所述整体中的生及继续生成的正面意义所说更多：活的任一读法都不含继续生发；生生作为方向之所向，以扩大相比较；C，即扩大可形成者更好，给出次序，一般而言只有它，亦不断言（07 §4）；不宣称任一前提已无前提得证<br>Stated on 7 October 2026 as a direction, in an answer, with no argument given; thesis T8 now argues it under both premises, with its warrant open (07 §8); G0, once a starting point, is no premise of the current account: living significance (every living claim has significance from every standpoint), the Lean `G0` under its present name (08 §10), follows under the continuation condition, which the theory does not assert (07 §3); the favorable significance of whole life and further generation that G0 stated says more: no reading of living includes further generation; 生生, the object of the direction, is compared by widening; and C, widening what can form is better, gives order, also not asserted (07 §4); no premise-free proof of either premise is claimed | [复核时的表述与范围 / Statement and scope as reviewed](../history/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#shengsheng-as-the-positive-ethical-direction)<br>[条件小节 / The conditional section](../history/THE-THEORY-OF-BECOMING.md#615-若任何东西的延续都有意义--if-the-continuation-of-anything-has-significance)<br>[来源：回答 / Source: the answer](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-shengsheng-ethical-direction.md) |
| C21 | 共同形成本身的候选正面意义<br>Co-formation itself as a candidate favorable realization | 新关系使原本不能共同发生的生活及进一步形成条件共同实现时，整体形成一种新的实现方式；其正面意义不必先成为发起者收益。 | When new relations enable forms of living and conditions for further formation that could not previously occur together to be jointly realized, the whole gains a new mode of realization; its favorable significance need not first become a return to the initiator. | 本表核心哲学候选；不是由共同可行性直接证明的普遍伦理身份。<br>The central philosophical candidate; universal ethical identity is not proved merely by joint feasibility. | [所引原段 / Quoted passage](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/ZERO-AXIOM-CLASSIFICATION-INTEGRATION.md#4-正面伦理推论得到更准确的对象但仍需要根据)<br>[完整正面论证 / Affirmative argument](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/ZERO-AXIOM-CLASSIFICATION-ARGUMENT.md#5-the-strongest-affirmative-ethical-hypothesis) |
| C05 | 不以回报发起者为善的条件<br>Goodness need not return to the initiator | 实际的好与后续生成可以发生在别处或发起者退出之后；无需先证明它最终回报一个扩大的自我。 | Actual good and further generation can occur elsewhere or after the initiator leaves; they need not first be justified as benefits returning to an enlarged self. | 被辩护的整体伦理立场；普遍效力待证<br>Defended whole ethical position; universal authority open | [复核时的表述与范围 / Statement and scope as reviewed](../history/NONDUAL-METHOD.md#begin-within-generative-unfolding)<br>[相关论证 / Related argument](../history/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#whole-appraisal-needs-no-additional-beneficiary) |
| C06 | 自身之善、休息与有限实现<br>Self-good, rest and finite realization | 自身照料、休息与有限完成属于整体中的真实实现，不必靠后续生产或额外外部收益才有地位。 | Self-care, rest and finite fulfillment are real realizations within the whole; they need not earn standing through later production or additional outward returns. | 明确保留的伦理范围；非生产义务<br>Explicitly retained ethical scope; no productivity duty | [复核时的表述与范围 / Statement and scope as reviewed](../history/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#self-care-is-a-realization-within-the-whole)<br>[相关论证 / Related argument](../history/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#renewal-and-practical-importance-do-not-exhaust-goodness) |
| C22 | 方向、持续能力与最大化的区别<br>Direction, continuing capacity and maximization | 生生方向不要求每一步增长、每种旧形式保留或最大化单一数字；真实新生、有限实现与损失在同一历史中判断。 | The generative direction requires neither growth at every step, preservation of every old form nor maximization of a single number; novelty, finite fulfillment and losses are assessed within one history. | 当前方向的范围限定；没有预设单调性、全序或永续。<br>Scope qualification of the current direction; no assumed monotonicity, total ordering or endless persistence. | [正面方向及限定 / Direction and qualifications](../history/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#shengsheng-as-the-positive-ethical-direction)<br>[前提与选择的检查 / Premise and selection audit](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/GENERATIVE-PREMISE-INTEGRATION.md) |
| C30 | 生成关系的解释必要性<br>Generative relations and explanatory necessity | 独立指定的取得、错误与后续问题要求保留实际差异和共同边界；接合探针进一步恢复有限片段接口，三元关系唯一确定局部单位，完整有限轨迹恢复其状态接口。整体表述仍合格，容量反例限定接口匹配的充分性。 最新从原有机制直接证明摘要充分性，推出有效规律与全部有限历史精确对应，不另给Exact或同态前提。 普遍化检验给出任意三元关系恢复的充要条件；完整成功答案与最少状态／边仍允许不同构机制，确定且可达、后续可区分时恢复唯一对应。 后续以共同条件下的复合接续问题推出有限分支双模拟，并由准确约简答案构造可从每个相容原生起点提升的编码规律；结构唯一性不再要求确定性。 再以后继紧致性放宽有限分支，取得结构对应及完整无限编码路径提升；有无限多个可区分后继的正例，也有全部有限复合答案相同而无限持续不同的反例。 共同问题再在明示成对紧致性下迫出步骤／接合对应并提升有限逐节点方案；实际占用／释放推出共享冲突。全部孤立共同问题仍可能遗漏指定部分的共享身份，固定搭配问题在有限实例中恢复。 再以完整下层支持覆盖及同级独占支持分离，将任意有限方案接到全局无重复与一次共同占用历史；遗漏关系的失败及修复正例均检查。每个固定 n 阶成功清单仍可漏掉 n+1 阶共享容量限制。 变化中的形成、撤去、占用与释放具有精确证书保持条件；任意允许有限历史在同一原生规律内都有完成后缀。静态清单会遗漏实际占用，释放／形成可恢复或建立准备；无限允许调度仍可能永不推进目标。 全体有限一阶身份、生成和接合真值一致，甚至有一个保留任意有限参数公式的映射，仍不足以迫出全范围可达结构的原生同构；Cantor 对角线给出真实不同构对手。保真映射再有全局覆盖则恢复同构。 纠正：原反例未对准共同必要生成基础，不能作为原目标停止证据；给定位置之间可达未解释其构成性形成。 纠正后由同一部分接合关系形成全部有限情境，准确说明必须保留改变接续可能性的区别，冗余说明亦有共同因子；固定片段接合结果唯一时，共同内容带有唯一对应的操作。实际历史的全部嵌套接合问题恰好保留开始／结束接口，不抹去不同内部事件。 后续允许接合本身有多个结果：在排中律下，有限结果纤维及更丰富的分支问答迫出共同接合关系，原指定输入能实现每个代码结果；描述的共同核是接合的互模拟时，二者无需前提即成立；检验伙伴随同一编码转换，任意有限接合树提升。共同表示仍可有两个可区分结果。 不共用片段载体时，经关联角色相似的根无需有限或判定即可导出单位置接合的共同细化及与既有投影相容的唯一保接合因子；在排中律及局部有限下，关联同测的根亦然；但无引用关联问题会使所有有局部单位的片段同测，遗漏已消耗历史的不可自接合。保留同一配置的两个引用位置后，跨载体的有限形成历史可以匹配，精确说明必区分同一过程的空历史与已消耗历史。 有限宏步骤由同一过程路径形成，完整宏问答可精确翻译；在排中律下，接收侧有限分支及翻译问答一致迫出不同原生步数的有限历史匹配。全部非空前缀问答仍可遗漏中途失续；在实际到达位置开放后缀检验可恢复区别。 有限条件程序进一步按已到达条件分支或提前结束；宏问答恢复之外，相同语法与相似的起点可匹配每个部分历史并保留余项；在排中律及接收侧有限分支下，全部原生问答同测亦然。完整执行恰好等于展开到结束；同起点同原生位置的余项差异迫使精确说明区分。插入活动可使待用接续失效，实际前中后段仍接为一条历史。 | Independently specified acquisition, error and continuation questions require native distinctions and shared boundaries. Joining probes further recover finite-fragment interfaces; the ternary relation determines unique unit roles, and complete finite traces recover their state interfaces. Global accounts remain eligible; capacity limits matching-unit sufficiency. The latest concrete summary-sufficiency proof derives an effective law and exact finite-history correspondence from the unchanged mechanism, without a separate Exact or homomorphism premise. The universal attempt characterizes arbitrary ternary-relation recovery: complete success answers and minimum states/edges still allow nonisomorphic mechanisms; deterministic reachable continuation-separated realizations recover uniqueness. The continuation derives finite-branch bisimulation from compound questions at shared conditions and reconstructs code histories lifting from every compatible native start; scoped structural uniqueness no longer requires determinism. Successor compactness further replaces finite branching, preserving structural correspondence and lifting whole infinite code runs; a genuinely infinitely branching positive model contrasts with equal finite-modal answers but different infinite realization. Joint questions further force step/joint correspondence under explicit correlated-pair compactness and lift finite nodewise plans; acquisition/release derives shared-support conflict. Complete isolated joint profiles can still lose designated-part aliasing, recovered by fixed-partner questions in the finite witness. Complete descendant support and sibling exclusion further connect arbitrary finite plans to distinct global requests and a common holding history; omission failures and a positive repair are checked. Every fixed n-ary success list can still miss an (n+1)-ary capacity restriction. Formation, withdrawal, acquisition and release have exact certificate-preservation conditions; every permitted finite history has a completing suffix in the same native law. Static lists can lose actual occupation, release/formation renew readiness, and an infinite permitted schedule can still never progress. All finite first-order identity, step and joint truths, even preserved by one coherent elementary map on every finite source tuple, do not force native isomorphism of wholly root-reachable worlds. Cantor gives a genuine obstruction; adding global coverage yields isomorphism. Corrected: the counterexample is not accepted as a stopping test for a common necessary generative basis: reachability between supplied positions does not explain their constitution. After correction, all finite contexts arise from the same partial joining law. Exact accounts must retain differences affecting continuation and have a common factor even when redundant. Functional fragment joining induces a common operation. All nested joining questions on admitted histories recover exactly their beginning/ending interface while internal event differences remain real. The continuation permits multivalued joining itself: under excluded middle, finite outcome fibers and richer branch questions force a common joining relation, and each code result lifts from the originally specified inputs; where the descriptions' one kernel is a bisimulation of the joining, both hold with no premise. Partners in questions translate through the same encoding, and every finite joining tree lifts. The common account can retain two distinguishable outcomes. Across different fragment carriers, roots alike through their incidence roles yield, with no finiteness or decision, a common refinement with one-position lifting and a unique join-preserving factor commuting with given projections; roots agreeing on every incidence question do so under excluded middle with local finiteness. Unanchored incidence questions nevertheless collapse all local-unit fragments, losing the spent history’s impossible self-join. Retaining two reference slots in the same configuration recovers matched finite formation histories; exact accounts must distinguish idle and spent histories within one process. Finite blocks arise from paths of the same process and all block questions compile exactly. Under excluded middle, receiving-side finite branching and translated-answer agreement match histories with different native lengths. All nonempty-prefix questions can still omit intermediate loss; suffix tests at the actual reached position restore the distinction. Finite guarded recipes branch or end at reached conditions. Beyond macro recovery, common syntax and alike starts match partial histories with residuals retained, as does complete native-answer agreement under excluded middle with receiving-side finite branching. Execution equals unfolding to skip; different residuals at one reached native position force exact accounts to distinguish them. Inserted activity can disable a pending continuation while the actual joined history remains. | 此前各轮及实际展开42项定理／64项新审计保留原范围与独立评审；本轮另54项（23＋31）／128项含继承审计：105空、23仅propext。探针可用性、状态内容及完整接续条件明示；共享容量／并行冲突下的定义域与输出识别仍待推进。 新增14项定理／18项空依赖审计，另192项继承依赖不变（150空、42标准）；独立审查接受。既有温控／氧梯度证据接入，但不等于该构造的物理实现或普遍根基完成；重叠继承不累加。 此独立一轮61项新定理／135项审计（123空、12继承propext；新61项全空）。已有14项有效规律推导保留；普遍目标为何必须区分相关原生关系仍未证，有限反例不整体否定普遍猜想。 此轮45项新定理／141项审计：103空、38标准；新19空、26标准，其中13含经典选择。有限分支、问题覆盖、约简／状态区分分别明示；前向充分性不恢复全部实际形成史，普遍解释范围仍未证。 此轮36项新定理／159项审计（102空、57标准；新5空、31标准）。后继紧致性是显式充分条件，非普遍定律或有限观察结论；完整持续、实际历史及一般共同接合各保留范围。 本轮56项新定理／183项审计（112空、71标准；新6空、50标准）。成对紧致性、问题覆盖与分离明示；逐节点共同方案不等于任意部分替换或全局并发，普遍唯一性仍未证。 此轮37项新定理／184项审计（115空、69标准；新4空、33标准）。固定接入、取得后保留及操作对应明示；支持范围可为过宽充分证书，不把相交自动当作失败。一般动态共同实现与普遍唯一性未证。 本轮42项新定理／211项审计（119空、92标准；新6空、36标准，无新增经典选择依赖）。连续准备比修复后的最终成功更强；原子步骤、全部相关变化受控与真实状态读取明示，普遍唯一性未证。 本轮33项新定理／180项审计（113空、67标准；新2空、31标准，其中13含经典选择）。一阶内容到全局覆盖的推论遇到已证明边界；不是对全部可能真理充分，也不否定不同实现共享必要生成基础。普遍根基唯一性未证。 当前状态修正：保留全部已证结论，撤回共同根基研究已撞墙的判断，不再预设全局双射为必要门槛；继续检验同一生成中分化与充分说明所必需的关系。本次无新证明，非二元亦不等于普遍唯一性已证。 本轮38项新定理／166项审计（125空、41标准；新20空、18标准，无新增经典选择）。原过程可分支，确定的是固定历史的接合；规律、语义可能性与实际取得检验分列。普遍解释对应仍待证明，不再以完整世界同构或当前未证连接宣布原目标撞墙。 新26项声明（含适配与应用，非26项独立发现）／223项审计：新6空、20标准，8项含经典选择；197项继承审计另列。删除单值条件同时加强检验并限定结果纤维，不声称原生身份、概率、共享资源或任意解释的普遍适用；下一连接是不同关系分节的有据互译。 本轮43项新声明／256项审计（213继承；新9空、34标准，其中7含经典选择）。关联细化用连通，以关联同测的根时另用局部有限及排中律；引用修复用初始位置数相同，并用相似的起点，或用完整问答一致、固定输入对有限结果及排中律。不给予物理复制许可，不把关系图路径当作时间执行；实际表述对应和步骤粒度变化仍待论证。 2026-10-09：23项新声明／279项闭包审计，256项继承另列；新23标准依赖、3含经典选择。固定有限展开、问答一致、排中律与实际对应明示；单步覆盖接通约简核，是充分条件。可变展开、中间引用互译及普遍唯一性仍未证。 新35项声明／314项审计，279继承另列；新12空、23标准，9含经典选择。所有新增数学结论及失败见证均有Lean对应；语义判断不等于实际传感，程序余项不另立本体。任意内部语法互译与普遍根据仍待证明。<br>Earlier studies, including native unfolding (42 theorems / 64 new audits and its independent review), retain their scopes. This separate 54 (23+31) / 128 audit study has 105 empty and 23 propext-only lists. Probe access, state content and completion are explicit; identification with shared-capacity/concurrent conflicts remains open. New 14 theorems / 18 empty audits; 192 inherited maps unchanged (150 empty, 42 standard), independently reviewed. Existing controller/oxygen evidence is integrated without equating those cases with this construction or completing universal grounding. Overlapping imports are not added. This separate round adds 61 theorems / 135 audits (123 empty, 12 inherited propext; all new 61 empty). Existing effective-law results remain credited. Universal separation of native relations remains unwarranted; the scoped counterexample does not globally refute the conjecture. This round: 45 new theorems / 141 audits, 103 empty and 38 standard; new 19 empty and 26 standard, including 13 with classical choice. Finite branching, question coverage and reduction/separation are explicit; forward adequacy does not recover every actual past or prove universal explanatory scope. This round adds 36 theorems / 159 audits (102 empty, 57 standard; new 5 empty, 31 standard). Successor compactness is an explicit sufficient condition, not a universal law or finite-observation inference; whole continuation, actual history and general joint realization retain distinct scopes. This round adds 56 theorems / 183 audits (112 empty, 71 standard; new 6 empty, 50 standard). Pair compactness, question coverage and separation are explicit; nodewise joint plans do not establish arbitrary substitution or global concurrency, and universal uniqueness remains unproved. This round adds 37 theorems / 184 audits (115 empty, 69 standard; new 4 empty, 33 standard). Fixed access, persistent holding and operational correspondence are explicit; overapproximating footprints are sufficient certificates, and overlap alone is not failure. General dynamic joint realization and universal uniqueness remain unproved. This round adds 42 theorems / 211 audits (119 empty, 92 standard; new 6 empty, 36 standard, no new classical-choice dependency). Continuous readiness is stronger than eventual success after repair; atomic steps, full mediation and current-state access are explicit, and universal uniqueness remains unproved. This round adds 33 theorems / 180 audits (113 empty, 67 standard; new 2 empty and 31 standard, 13 with classical choice). The inference from first-order content to global coverage has a proved wall. This is not unrestricted truth adequacy or a refutation of a shared necessary generative foundation; universal foundation uniqueness remains open. Corrected current status: retain every proved result, withdraw the stopping judgment for common-foundation uniqueness, and remove the unearned global-bijection gate. Investigate constituted distinctions and necessary explanatory relations within one unfolding. No new proof; nonduality does not establish universality. This round adds 38 theorems / 166 audits (125 empty, 41 standard; new 20 empty and 18 standard, no added classical choice). The underlying process may branch; fixed-history joining is functional. Laws, semantic possibility and acquired inquiry are distinguished. Universal explanatory correspondence remains open, without treating whole-world isomorphism or this unproved bridge as a stopping wall. New 26 declarations (including adapters/applications, not 26 independent discoveries) / 223 audits: new 6 empty and 20 standard, 8 with classical choice; 197 inherited audits are separate. Removing functionality enriches tests and constrains fibers. Native identity, probabilities, shared resources and applicability to every explanation are not claimed; the next connection is warranted translation across different relational articulations. This round: 43 new declarations / 256 audits (213 inherited; new 9 empty and 34 standard, 7 with classical choice). Incidence refinement uses connectivity, and from roots agreeing on incidence questions also local finiteness and excluded middle; reference repair uses equal initial slot counts, with alike starts, or with complete answer agreement, finite fixed-pair outcomes and excluded middle. It licenses no physical copying and does not identify graph paths with temporal execution. Actual correspondence and operation-granularity changes remain open. 2026-10-09: 23 new declarations / 279 closure audits, 256 inherited separate; all new use standard dependencies, 3 include classical choice. Fixed finite expansion, complete question agreement, excluded middle and actual correspondence remain explicit. Singleton coverage is sufficient to align reduced kernels; variable expansion, intermediate-reference translation and universal uniqueness remain open. New 35 declarations / 314 audits, 279 inherited separate; new 12 empty and 23 standard, 9 with classical choice. Every new mathematical claim and failure witness has a Lean counterpart. Semantic guards are not actual sensors; residuals introduce no separate ontology. | C09 | 形成能力、实际运用与接续<br>Formed capacity, actual exercise and continuation | 能力已形成、能力实际被运用、下轮条件得到再形成是不同关系；任意高的有限完成可能，不能仅凭这些替代可能性推出一条实际持续形成的历史。 固定规律下的旧可达性区分现细化为共同实现的准备步骤界：实际准备可改变当前一步能力，解除共同条件可失去最终共同实现，已有能力也可能未被使用。 进一步区别形态保留与有支持的作用：维持活动可要求改变支承路径，恢复一项条件可伴随另一项耗竭；形成、维持、转化、退化与结束须按实际关系同时说明。 明示共同操作还可在双方完整局部状态不变时改变后续响应；保留相应交叉项可准确跟随指定操作的任意有限序列。 同一支持门控规律还区分仍受支持的有限修复与双失支持后的停滞：后续输入表相同不保证同样恢复。 | Formed capacity, its actual exercise and renewed next-stage conditions are distinct; arbitrarily high finite completion possibilities do not by themselves establish one history of ongoing actual formation. The existing fixed-law reachability distinction now has an explicit preparation bound for joint realization: actual preparation can change current one-step capacity, release can remove eventual joint realization, and available capacity can remain unused. The continuation distinguishes retained form from supported efficacy: maintaining an activity can require changing its provision route, and restoring one condition can exhaust another. Formation, maintenance, transformation, degeneration and ending are explained through their actual relationships. A specified joint operation can also change later responses while both complete local states stay fixed; retaining the relevant cross contribution gives exact responses along every finite word of the declared operations. The same support-gated renewal law distinguishes finite repair through a still-supported route from inactivity after both supports are lost: equal later input schedules do not ensure equal recovery. | 原始延时研究进一步记录界面小筏形成、下落和再形成；酸碱条件改变空间组织，须与持续膜结构和实际后代分别对应。 此前比较方向提出独立机制约束下的跨条件接续，以及重构和选择关系自身的改变。 新的24小时形成实测与常规模型进一步接通建立条件和留存占比；较低离散的优势取决于相对生长，反例保留。 已完成的演化组织说明进一步接通实际取样、继承发育状态与跨转移重建；组织改变后续取样结果，真实供给及后续修订均保留。 原始显微数据现核对真实尺度及逐位置拍摄时钟，混合培养两段采集的位置配置可接续；成像对应与培养物身份仍分别论证。 直接同等网络比较现证明重构机制具有精确的常规实现，包含依当前状态选择及失败行动。 转而聚焦理论，系应请求，未给论证；比较前提与额外解释不需要新的测量，所以无新测量也可进行：复用形成、重构、更新器及目的修订成果，比较其独立前提与额外解释，当时选定具体联结论学习结果的恢复。 预先固定视野的91帧现接成实测发育史；增长后的局部消失与轨迹的边界删失分别保留，观察结束不独自说明结构丧失。 新理论连接把后继重建能力接到完整回返路径的选择阈值及繁殖代价；直接外形延续可下降，第一代相同产出仍可掩盖相反的增长条件。 现已恢复单步Hebb方向，有限循环发育的后果响应由规律导出；tanh符号相容条件保持方向，并支持有限关系改变，反馈和选择关系改变可反转局部增益。 有限组装程序现从实际消耗及成功停止共同推出P列产出和重建比例；成功概率不同于后继比例，额外尝试可提高成功却消除增长优势。既有类型的增长因子接入相邻规则入侵条件。 新的共同历史由逐项非线性选择推出留存关系：同一网络从不完整线索重现两种格局，持续偏向一方造成旧格局失去重现能力，继续修订又能恢复；当前选择改善不保证保留旧能力。 实际传递组成与保留接触机会现导出局部成功率，后继自行重建成键；同一亲代、同平均成键率和接触消耗仍可因后继间机会分布不同而产生不同重建。不同种类传播体的响应不能无条件压成同一个回返系数。 后续同史构造把反复干扰造成的实际反应失败接到更新器维持与替换：由原生发育算出回报差别，保留旧关系与剩余支持，新规则实际改变后续更新并取得维持所需支持。 三阶段接续现证明条件性的有限周期回返，平衡混合时可用发育后响应的均值合并；成熟亲代的准备规律仍须实现。 组成生成与付费安放现实现指定亲代准备：同类生成使原先接触重建优势倒转，互补生成又改变排序；失败保留未用组成和支持，未完成指定返回不等于死亡。 参考接触现取得未知仿射通道的实际读数，形成的局部比较与抑制连接推出响应式更新，并在保留网络及支持记录中运行；校准后的通道变化可造成实际误报并阻止修复。 新参考比较现决定保留或修订已取得的读数，并实际恢复原网络更新；有限工作段之间可以改变通道。仿射通道零点相同、增益同向恰好保留全部符号判断，参数变化不独自等于能力丧失。 生长与断键现从实际连接图导出后继成员，接入共同材料准备；同组成及同边数可有不同共同传递，均匀断键与最老边先断使排序反转。新形成的替代维持关系还可使原有断键不再导致群体分裂。 实际逐步变化与噪声现推出取得读数的误差界，原生网络推出反应余量；工作段内通道可持续改变而在充分年龄窗口内保留实际修复及后续两类记忆。区间跨界保留不确定，可靠性取决于关系变化与辨别余量的共同条件。 实际安放材料现经逐项接合生成链，断裂保留内部边，片段经同一规律重建为可再分割链；任意有限轮次在共同材料和支持预算下实际接续，失败保留未完成后继及余料。 社会组织接续现由共同工作形成资格，经实际提案与授权改变规则，分化后继承的程序改变后续筛选；规则复制与实际参与分开，技术接续可保持而规则修订能力失去。 接续由共同活动中分化、相互维持和改变的反馈解释，因为本理论的方法不从各自给定、事后才连接的部分开始（08 §1）；工作、能力、参与及边界变化尚未由局部程序证明闭合。 新组合将实际尝试、回应与修订后的重试接入既有审查和接纳程序；形成的参与关系继续产生下一次回应，并在最初回应者离开后接续。规则继承与实践训练是门控改变之律的实例：量沿每段非门控动作的进程保持，恰当每个非门控步保持它；所以每段没有采纳的历史都继承规则，每次规则改变都位于一次采纳，训练沿每段不经修订、回到该情境的进程保持。 新结构复制接通真实交换、逐项接合和有限谱系：保留排列改变下一轮所需支持，两个排列的成本约束第三个；共同供给中的次序能改变哪型先完成。结构复制、接触、纠正与链组装机都是对每个相等可判定的字母表与每个汲取律成立的一般复制律的实例：复制产生一词，当且仅当池供给其记号且支持覆盖其汲取；自同一共同池依次复制的各词全部释放，当且仅当池与支持共同覆盖它们，与次序无关；在某位对照参考检查不增加缺陷，当且仅当参考与模板在该位都有记号时二者一致。 接触现在由消耗连接件与支持的对接形成，实际读取随缺接、错位和重接而变化；恢复读取不改写已成前缀，变异后继也能被忠实续传。 新形成的检查／编辑关系进一步把旧错误产物接到实际材料替换、释放及后继；发现差异、改正和继续复制的支持门槛分开，错误参考能掩盖或制造错误。 | Consequences can change successor formation and selection without guaranteeing contributor advantage. Reconstruction, development and aggregation distinguish inherited clusters from transport groups; bacterial reversion links switching to cycle completion. Lineage forecasts retain aggregate gains and local failures. Primary evidence now links respiration-generated oxygen gradients to changed selection; structural development must enter transferable prediction. Conventional explanation receives full credit. Original time-lapse research adds recurring surface rafts and pH-sensitive spatial organization, requiring separate correspondences for persistent mat structure and actual descendants. Earlier comparative work proposed continuation across conditions constrained by independent mechanisms, followed by changes in reconstruction and selection relations. New 24-hour observations and a conventional model connect establishment to retained representation; lower dispersal gains only under the relative-growth condition, with an adverse contrast preserved. The completed evolutionary-organization account now connects actual sampling, inherited developmental state and reconstruction across transfers; formed organization changes later sampling, with actual provision and subsequent revision retained. Original microscopy now supplies checked scale and position-specific capture times, with continued position configuration across two rescue acquisitions; imaging correspondence and culture identity remain distinct. A direct matched-network comparison now establishes an exact conventional realization of the reconstruction mechanism, including shared state-dependent selectors and failed actions. A theoretical focus follows, on request, with no argument given; comparing premises and added explanation needs no new measurement, so it can proceed without one: reuse formation, reconstruction, updater and goal-revision results, compare their independent premises and added explanation, and recover a specific connectionist learning result selected at the time. A predefined field now has a measured 91-frame developmental history; local disappearance after growth is distinguished from boundary censoring, so an observation ending does not alone establish structural loss. A new theoretical connection gives a full-cycle selection threshold and reproductive cost ceiling for inherited reconstruction; direct resemblance can fall, and identical first-generation outputs can conceal opposite growth conditions. The single-step Hebbian direction is now recovered. Finite recurrent sensitivity follows from the developmental law; a sign-compatible tanh regime preserves the direction through finite changes, while feedback and changed selective relations can reverse local gains. A finite assembly programme now derives P-column production and reconstruction jointly from paid attempts and stopping. Success probability differs from the descendant fraction, and added attempts can improve success while removing growth advantage. The resident factor enters an adjacent-rule invasion condition. A new common history derives retained relations from coordinate-wise nonlinear selection: the same network recalls two patterns from incomplete cues, continued emphasis on one destroys old recall, and further revision restores it. Current-score improvement does not guarantee preservation of an earlier capacity. Actual transmitted components and retained contact slots now determine local success, with each successor rebuilding its own bond. The same parent, mean encounter rate and cost can yield different reconstruction because opportunities are distributed differently across successors. Distinct seed responses cannot unconditionally be collapsed to one return coefficient. A subsequent common history connects actual response failures from repeated interference to updater maintenance and replacement: native development determines return differences, old relationships and remaining support persist, and the new rule governs later updates and earns renewal. A three-stage continuation proves conditional finite-cycle return and permits aggregation by the mean developed response under a balanced mixture; common mature-parent preparation still requires realization. Component production and paid routing now realize preparation of the specified parent: same-type production reverses the contact reconstruction ranking, while complementary production changes it again. Failed preparation retains components and work; missing the specified return does not establish death. Reference contacts now acquire actual readings of unknown affine channels. Formed local comparisons and inhibition derive responsive updating and operate in the retained network/support history; channel changes after calibration can cause false success and suppress repair. Fresh reference comparison now determines retention or revision of acquired readings and restores native updating across finite blocks with changed channels. Equal zero and same gain orientation exactly preserve the all-input affine sign interface; parameter change alone does not establish lost capacity. Growth and actual edge removal now derive successor membership from formed connections and join common material preparation. Equal composition and edge counts can yield different cotransmission; uniform and oldest-edge cuts reverse the ranking. A newly formed alternative retention route can also prevent a formerly separating cut from fragmenting the group. Actual successive changes and noise now imply acquired-reading error bounds, while native network responses imply a positive margin. Channels may keep changing within a block while actual repair and subsequent two-memory retention persist through a sufficient age window. Intervals crossing the boundary retain uncertainty; reliability depends jointly on relational change and discrimination margin. Actual placed material now forms a chain through tokenwise attachment; cleavage retains internal edges, and the fragments rebuild under the same law into cleavable chains. Every finitely supported number of rounds executes with shared material/work accounting; failure retains incomplete descendants and unused components. A social-organizational continuation now forms mandates through work, changes a rule through actual proposal and authorization, and inherits a procedure that changes later filtering; copied rules differ from actual participation, and technical continuation can persist while revision capacity is lost. Continuation is explained through differentiation and reciprocal maintenance/transformation within common activity, since the account's method does not begin from parts given independently and joined afterward (08 §1); the local protocol proof does not close work, capability, participation or changing boundaries. A new composition connects actual attempts, responses and revised retries to the existing review/admission process; formed participation enables later response and continuation after the initial reviewer leaves. Rule inheritance and practice training are instances of the gated-change law: a quantity is kept along every course of ungated moves exactly when every ungated step keeps it, so every history without an adoption inherits the rule, every rule change is located at an adoption, and training is kept along every course without revision that returns to the context. Structural copying now joins an actual exchange, tokenwise attachment and finite lineages: retained order changes subsequent support requirements, two structural costs constrain a third, and common-stock order can change which variant completes first. The structural, contact, correction and chain-assembly machines are instances of general copying laws for every alphabet with decidable equality and every law of draws: a copy produces a word exactly when the pool supplies its tokens and the support covers what it draws; copies drawn in turn from one shared pool are all released exactly when pool and support cover them together, whatever their order; and inspecting a site against a reference never increases defects exactly when, wherever both have a token there, the reference agrees with the template. Contacts now form through connector- and support-consuming docking; actual reads change with loss, displacement and rebinding. Restored reading does not rewrite a built prefix, and an altered daughter can be faithfully transmitted. A newly formed checking/editing relation joins the prior erroneous product to actual material exchange, release and a successor; inspection, correction and continued copying have distinct support conditions, while wrong references can conceal or introduce error. | | C33 | 多系统试验与父子关系<br>Plural trials and parent/subsystem relations | 共同整体内可形成互补观察、可转移试验及分址纠正；系统群是否可作一个单位取决于实际组织、相关干预与共享依存。 关系接口可准确保留共同可行性，却仍遗漏依观察选择实现的条件；复合关系可被探测、修复并在内部修订中保持。 进一步区分完整端点关系与实际请求历史：逐段准备新路径，同时保护每个在途请求所引用的旧路径。 局部机制可以在其范围内保证某一效果，而这一范围未必在此后每次变化中都持续，所以所研究的是认知与处境变化之中的边界条件和方向；主词是因应转化（AI 所取的工作名），因为它不预设分开的纠正者与被纠正者；可纠正性保留为AI对齐读者桥接。 | A shared whole can sustain complementary observations, transferable trials and separately addressed correction; grouping depends on actual organization, relevant interventions and shared dependencies. Complete endpoint relations are further distinguished from actual request histories: prepare a new route incrementally while protecting each in-flight request’s referenced route. A local mechanism may guarantee an effect within its range without that range persisting through every later change, so the question studied is conditions and direction through changing understanding and circumstances; the primary term is responsive transformation (an AI working name), since it presupposes no separate corrector and corrected object; corrigibility remains an AI-alignment reader bridge. | 条件说明与37项新定理／58项审计完成，继承19/31分列。独立误差增益与共同故障、局部汇总失效及联合负载反例并存；实际全局划分、试验传递与包含范围仍需证据。 关系接续再增39项定理／55项审计：任意有限翻译链及成对修订有正面证明，分开更新保留中途失败；实际协调实现与独立原创性未证。 版本接续新增33项定理／62项审计，继承62项定理／100项审计分列；4,512个状态、63,168条转移、100次交接及80次受控线程试验通过。保留原子取得与 pin 登记、生命周期及有限资源前提；既有网络更新获归属，实际AI对应与独立原创性未证。 本次为解释修订，无新数学结果；既有条件证明、处境变化反例及仍有根据的责任保留，不把无条件保证设为方向完成门槛。<br>Conditional account and 37 new theorems / 58 audits complete, with inherited 19/31 separate. Independent-error gains coexist with common-failure, summary and joint-load counterexamples; actual global boundaries, transfer and containment require evidence. Physical coordinated implementation and independent novelty remain open. Versioned continuation adds 33 theorems / 62 audits, with inherited 62 theorems / 100 audits separate; 4,512 states, 63,168 transitions, 100 handoffs and 80 controlled thread trials pass. Atomic acquisition with pin registration, lifetime and finite-resource premises remain; established network updates retain credit, while actual AI correspondence and independent novelty remain open. This is an interpretive revision with no new mathematical result. Existing conditional proofs, changed-circumstance failures and warranted responsibilities remain; unconditional assurance is not the direction’s completion gate. | [复核时的理论 / Account as reviewed](../history/PLURAL-SYSTEMS-AND-CORRECTION.md)<br>[整合、证明与反审 / Integration, proofs and counterreview](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/PLURAL-CORRECTION-INTEGRATION.md)<br>[来源：方向 / Source: the direction](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-08-plural-systems-and-parent-correction.md)<br>[关系接续、证明与反审 / Relational continuation, proofs and counterreview](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/RELATIONAL-CORRECTION-INTEGRATION.md)<br>[请求历史与版本接续 / Request histories and versioned continuation](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/VERSIONED-CORRECTION-INTEGRATION.md)<br>[复核时的因应转化表述 / Responsive-transformation account as reviewed](../history/PLURAL-SYSTEMS-AND-CORRECTION.md#15-responsive-transformation) |

## 三、伦理根据、真实取舍与情境理由 / Ethical warrant, real tradeoffs and situated reasons

| 编号 / ID | 理论或命题 / Theory or proposition | 中文论断 | English formulation | 范围与状态 / Scope and status | 原文、证明与评审 / Sources, proofs and reviews |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| C16 | 有根据的修正与学习<br>Warranted revision through learning | 学习应允许根据真实变化与证据修正旧判断，同时保留对实际根据的负责；封闭异议或保持旧判决都不等于正确。 | Learning must allow correction through actual changes and evidence while remaining answerable to the grounds; silencing challenges or freezing old verdicts does not establish correctness. | 被辩护的范围区分；普遍规范不变量待证<br>Defended scope distinction; universal normative invariant open | [复核时的表述与范围 / Statement and scope as reviewed](../history/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#justified-revision-and-the-current-sufficiency-limit) |
| C17 | 显式生成公理路线<br>The explicit generative-axiom route | 将整体中的生及继续生成的正面意义明确作为 G0 起点，系应请求，此处未给论证，推进共同的比较与情境推论；G0 曾作为 alignment 任务的公理采用，今不是现论的前提：存活之意义（每一个活的主张从每一个立场看都有意义），即今名之下的 Lean `G0`（08 §10），在延续条件之下得出（理论不断言，07 §3）；G0 所述整体中的生及继续生成的正面意义所说更多：活的任一读法都不含继续生发；生生作为方向之所向，以扩大相比较；C，即扩大可形成者更好，给出次序，一般而言只有它，亦不断言（07 §4）；任一前提的无前提证明都不是这一路线的完成门槛，因为只在所述前提之下报告结果的路线，需要写明前提，不需要证明前提。 | Take the favorable significance of whole life and further generation explicitly as G0, on request, with no argument given here, the starting point of common comparative and situated inferences; G0, once adopted as an axiom for the alignment task, is no premise of the current account: living significance (every living claim has significance from every standpoint), the Lean `G0` under its present name (08 §10), follows under the continuation condition, which the theory does not assert (07 §3); the favorable significance of whole life and further generation that G0 stated says more: no reading of living includes further generation; 生生, the object of the direction, is compared by widening; and C, widening what can form is better, gives order, also not asserted (07 §4); a premise-free proof of either premise is not a completion gate for this route, since a route that reports its results only under a stated premise needs that premise stated, not proved. | 明确公理路线的条件候选已完成并获独立接受；不声称起点无前提得证，也未证明其不可能，所以无前提的根据仍是开放目标。后续在实践判断聊天重新开启零公理目标，系应请求，未给论证，其他路线保留。<br>The conditional explicit-axiom candidate is complete and independently accepted; its starting point is not proved premise-free, nor is such a proof shown impossible, so a premise-free ground stays an open target. | C20 | 保留的零公理普遍元伦理目标<br>The preserved zero-axiom universal metaethical target | 更强历史目标试图从整体实际生成说明伦理质量及跨智能实践效力，并区分普遍有效、实际认可服从与表述的唯一必要性；当时的 alignment 任务改沿明示 G0 推导。 2026-10-13，当日研究会话以普遍规范奠基为重心，系应请求，未给论证；既有公理路线及P89局部结项保持。 当时的问法倾向于先分开中性事实、价值及独立的回应位置，再求其外部连接，而本理论的方法不从此开始：在把两项当作各自给定之前，先重构所说连接二者的关系（08 §1）。所以改问：在同一活动中说明规范分化，保留普遍目标。 | The stronger historical target seeks ethical quality and practical force across intelligence from actual whole generation, distinguishing universal validity, recognition/compliance and unique necessity of a formulation; the alignment task of that time instead proceeded from explicit G0. The question as then put tended to separate neutral fact, value and independent positions of response and then ask for an external link between them, which is not how the account's method begins: before two terms are treated as independently given, the relation said to join them is reconstructed (08 §1). ## 四、学习、关切、良知与智慧怎样形成 / Formation of learning, concern, Liangzhi and wisdom

| 编号 / ID | 理论或命题 / Theory or proposition | 中文论断 | English formulation | 范围与状态 / Scope and status | 原文、证明与评审 / Sources, proofs and reviews |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M01 | 依存、运作意义与调节<br>Dependency, operational significance and regulation | 组织所依存的关系改变其后续活动；当后果进入回应并改变支承条件时，调节便在同一过程中形成。 | Dependencies change what an organization can do next; regulation forms when consequences enter responses that alter those enabling relations. | 条件性机制与应用综合已接受；运作意义不等于体验或伦理价值。<br>Conditional mechanism and application synthesis accepted; operational significance is not experience or ethical value. | [依存、运作意义与调节 / Dependency, operational significance and regulation](../history/GENERATIVE-DEVELOPMENT.md) |
| M02 | 选择器及修订机制的形成<br>Formation of selectors and their revision mechanisms | 决定回应的机制本身可以形成、损耗、替换，并通过实际后果影响下一轮重建。 | Mechanisms selecting responses can themselves form, wear and be replaced, with actual consequences shaping subsequent reconstruction. | 有限条件构造与检查完成；备选规则、组装配方和提案律仍给定。<br>Finite conditional construction checked; alternative rules, assembly recipes and proposal laws remain supplied. | [选择器及修订机制的形成 / Formation of selectors and their revision mechanisms](../history/UPDATER-FORMATION.md)<br>[相关接续 / Related continuation](../history/SELECTOR-REVISION.md) |
| M03 | 后果学习、预测与行动<br>Consequence learning, prediction and action | 实际行动形成证据与留存记录；新证据可纠正预测并改变后续命令，但准确预测不保证行动有效。 | Actual action produces evidence and retained records; new evidence can correct prediction and change later commands, while accuracy alone does not ensure useful action. | 有限构造经 Lean 与独立数值重建检查；感知、解码及行动耦合的局部法则仍显式。<br>Finite construction checked in Lean and independent reconstruction; local sensing, decoding and action-coupling laws remain explicit. | [后果学习、预测与行动 / Consequence learning, prediction and action](../history/PREDICTION-ACTION.md) |
| M04 | 学习方式的后果性修订<br>Consequence-based revision of how learning occurs | 取得的诊断证据能够改变以后解释后果的机制，同时保留既有记录并传递修订结果。 | Acquired diagnostic evidence can change the mechanism interpreting later outcomes while retaining existing records and transmitting the revision. | 有限接口已检查；也能保留错误更新器，不保证演化改善学习。<br>Finite interface checked; it can also preserve a wrong updater and does not guarantee improving learning. | [学习方式的后果性修订 / Consequence-based revision of how learning occurs](../history/LEARNING-ROUTE-REVISION.md) |
| M05 | 后继学习与纠正的传递<br>Successor learning and transmission of correction | 实际形成的后继可以取得新证据、纠正继承的回应，并让下一代继承该纠正。 | An actually formed successor can acquire new evidence, correct an inherited response and transmit that correction to a further successor. | 有限谱系连接已检查；复制、运作与留存各需满足条件，错误回应也可被保存。<br>Finite lineage connection checked; copying, operation and retention have distinct conditions, and incorrect responses can also survive. | [后继学习与纠正的传递 / Successor learning and transmission of correction](../history/SUCCESSOR-LEARNING.md) |
| M06 | 对受影响关系的理解与纠正<br>Understanding and correcting recipient-directed action | 某处有效的回应可能在另一处失败；按实际关系区分记录，可以纠正后者而不覆盖前者的知识。 | A response effective in one relation can fail in another; context-specific records can correct the latter without overwriting the former knowledge. | 信息要求、有限实现与比较已检查；帮助改善可以没有私人资源增益。<br>Information requirements, finite realization and comparisons checked; improved help can yield no additional private resources. | [对受影响关系的理解与纠正 / Understanding and correcting recipient-directed action](../history/RECIPIENT-CORRECTION.md) |
| M07 | 证据、理由及修订的实际接续<br>Actual continuity of evidence, reasons and revision | 保留的证据经当前语境解释支持理由；撤回失效路径时，独立根据与实际重建条件必须保留。 | Retained evidence supports reasons through current contextual interpretation; withdrawing a defeated route must preserve independent grounds and actual reconstruction conditions. | 有限记录—理由—接续构造已验证；校准、事实到理由的关联及根理由仍需根据。<br>Finite record–reason–succession construction verified; calibration, fact-to-reason relevance and root reasons still need warrant. | [证据、理由及修订的实际接续 / Actual continuity of evidence, reasons and revision](../history/RECORDED-REASONS.md)<br>[相关接续 / Related continuation](../history/RECIPIENT-REASON-REVISION.md) |
| M08 | 取得的后果进入评价与选择<br>Acquired consequences entering evaluation and choice | 新取得的后果记录能够形成留存的评价影响，并改变为同一受影响者采取的实际行动。 | Newly acquired consequence records can form retained evaluative influence and change actual action for the same recipient. | 有限连接经 Lean 与独立重建接受；评价形成不独自证明普遍伦理有效性。<br>Finite connection accepted through Lean and independent reconstruction; formed evaluation does not itself prove universal ethical validity. | [取得的后果进入评价与选择 / Acquired consequences entering evaluation and choice](../history/EVALUATIVE-UPTAKE.md) |
| M09 | 评价影响、后继与新证据<br>Evaluative influence, succession and fresh evidence | 后继继承实际取得的评价影响，并以新证据修正手段、选择和实施行动。 | A successor inherits actually acquired evaluative influence and uses fresh evidence to revise means, choose and act. | 有限分阶段连接已接受；配方、校准、支持及伦理应用范围仍显式。<br>Finite staged connection accepted; recipes, calibration, support and ethical application remain explicit. | [评价影响、后继与新证据 / Evaluative influence, succession and fresh evidence](../history/EVALUATIVE-SUCCESSION.md) |
| M10 | 仁、感应、理解与实践的共同形成<br>Co-formation of ren, responsiveness, understanding and practice | 关切可以在共同活动中形成，并以实际受阻、缓解和展开纠正回应，而不只追求私人回报。 | Concern can form through shared activity and let actual obstruction, relief and development correct responses beyond private returns. | 构成解释、偏蔽复通及局部模型接口已发展；最小根基到仁的完整推导未完成。<br>Constitutive account, distortion/restoration analysis and local interfaces developed; the complete derivation of ren from the minimal root remains unfinished. | [仁、感应、理解与实践的共同形成 / Co-formation of ren, responsiveness, understanding and practice](../history/NONINSTRUMENTAL-CONCERN.md) |
| M11 | 目的纠正与新继续方式<br>Purpose correction and new ways to continue | 达到终点不等于维持活动的全过程条件；若旧路径没有合格完成，仅筛选旧路径不能创造它。 | Reaching an endpoint does not ensure an activity's conditions held throughout; filtering old paths cannot create a qualified completion absent from them. | 一般条件定理已实际检查；给定活动的实现正确性不证明该活动正当。<br>General conditional theorems checked; correct realization of a specified activity does not prove its justification. | [目的纠正与新继续方式 / Purpose correction and new ways to continue](../history/NONINSTRUMENTAL-CONCERN.md) |
| M12 | 本然良知的生成演化与发展<br>Generative evolution and development of liangzhi | 先前活动改变发育、交往和再形成条件，使感应、照料与纠正能力能够传递和重建。 | Earlier activity changes developmental, social and reproductive conditions, allowing responsiveness, care and correction capacities to be transmitted and reconstructed. | 19项传递与17项发展声明已检查，有局部原始研究支持；完整良知历史与普遍可靠性未证。<br>Nineteen transmission and seventeen development declarations checked, with bounded primary evidence; a complete liangzhi history and universal reliability remain unproved. | [本然良知的生成演化与发展 / Generative evolution and development of liangzhi](../history/LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION.md) |
| M13 | 良知的活动辨别与校准<br>Activity-sensitive discernment and calibration of liangzhi | 回应须辨明物品、手段及请求在实际共同活动中的作用，而非固定服从或固定物品评价。 | Responses must discern the role of objects, means and requests in actual shared activity rather than follow fixed obedience or object rankings. | 两项幼儿原始研究及旧证明对应经过限定评估；未证明完整发展史或伦理目的修订。<br>Two primary toddler studies and correspondence with earlier proofs assessed within bounds; no complete developmental history or ethical-purpose revision established. | [良知的活动辨别与校准 / Activity-sensitive discernment and calibration of liangzhi](../history/LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION.md) |
| M14 | 关切的变通、交接与有限完成<br>Responsive concern, handoff and finite completion | 手段、目的、知情参与和既有承担的改变会改变回应；实际交接与合理结束也能参与接续。 | Changes in means, purposes, informed participation and undertakings change responses; actual handoff and justified endings can participate in continuation. | 20项交接、失败、恢复及有限结束声明已检查；依存不独自分配责任，成功交接不独自认证仁。 新 J3/J4 另行说明责任及分工；机制成功不自动赋予权限。<br>Twenty handoff, failure, recovery and finite-ending declarations checked; dependence alone allocates no duty, and successful handoff alone does not establish ren. New J3/J4 separately address responsibility and allocation; mechanism success does not confer authority. | [关切的变通、交接与有限完成 / Responsive concern, handoff and finite completion](../history/LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION.md)<br>[实践责任与结束 / Practical responsibility and discharge](../history/PRACTICAL-JUDGMENT.md) |
| M15 | 智慧的协同修正与慧根<br>Coordinated correction in wisdom and huigen | 智慧协同因果理解、有根据的关切与可实行回应，并能够修正事实、排除规则和阻断纠正的权力。 本轮接续说明同一要求可有相反回应，前次成功也可实际改变下一步的条件。 新候选解释把共同生成约束的可追踪展开作为智慧推演能力的来源；理由通过真实实现关系及公开规范根据获得内容。 后续回应必要性检验表明：同一过程中的充分辨别可以不同，且未必有仍充分的共同最粗描述；共同根不预设共同最小表述。 目的评价接续定位成就指标遗漏的实际保护关系：相同旧、新成就表可有相反比较，固定的关系敏感规则亦能满足要求。 有限判断接续表明，正确风险陈述可以伴随实际失败；必要性检验须匹配判断的内容与强度，单次成败不独自判定事前界限。 整个根的覆盖检验进一步区分具体判断、形成的能力与完整解释：共同根须解释智慧的分化及联系，不要求每次活动执行全部理论分支。 实质对照区分理由的支持、适用、认识与实行作用；承认具体约束不独自确立一套普遍根，须辨明可正当接续的共同关系。 道德所考虑的是变化之后的结果，故读作对becoming的判断，又因本理论的模型可以判断becoming，故读作由其普遍生成原则评价（这是读法，两项前提在此都未论证）；这些原则足以评价它在此未证，伦理的范围出于延续条件，次序出于 C，二者都不断言（07 §4）。不同回应与熟练者不显式循原则，不独自否定共同规范根据。 第15节从固定G0/J/E展开八种判断及其改变条件：共同原则既能支持相反回应，也能保留未被唯一决定的选择，并定位判断遗漏。 2026-10-13接续复用P75的已完成形式支撑：从独立陈述的判断内容、根据、范围与强度接到共同回应、推广和有界识别条件，分列理论理解、隐性技艺、认识范围、共同判断及道德判断；有根据的省略与有害遗漏得到区分。 第17节具体比较非表征的熟练实践说明：显式规则可省，实际联系仍须交代；既有常值描述边界要求先固定所问内容，再评价省略是否保真。 第18节接通放下与结束的实际内容：共同支持的改变与注意／体验的改变分列，必要关系容许旧形式结束；交接、解除及P91保真边界防止把遗漏真实需要当作改善。 第19节把理论智慧接到内容成立、根据与实际理解的关系，分开共同联系与分支条件；旧闭包／共同解释结果将全范围证明定位于根与规则的联合根据。 第20节将实际把握与发现路线分开：偶然发现后可读懂，直接领会无需显式推导，冗余根据可共同作用；既有习得绑定给出内容进入解释与执行的一条实际路线。 第21节以原代入与同值误接定位内容依存：必要保留针对所称成就，不是整个表示；核心接合约束同一中间配置的继续。 第22节核对整个core的接口：活动读法与实际支持／交换的保留各有已完成证明；成就内容须经确切对应接到所用关系，不能以可表示性或依存同名完成共同根论证。 第23节复用而不重建，因为一个理论之内已给出的连接不需要平行模型：四项纯核心适配直接连接P91的接纳内容与P93的选择保留，给出保真分组与整类所需选择保留的精确对应及遗失见证；P99的实际取得／修订／使用和P89的完成／正面根据作为已完成连接直接消费。 第24节新增四项适配，将P93直接接到任意Frame的主张成立状态，无需数值需要或可判定性；同一原过程的固定字面读法分开跨配置选择保留与同一配置中的主张保真。 §25接入构成性绑定、实际取得与P103受支持持续：同一运行继续指定任务并真实退役旧区别；当前内容根据、实际使用和后续支承在共同活动中分化。 §26接入原可错辨别及及时回应必要性：有覆盖与错误界的能力仍需足够差异进入使用；不同内部描述、暂缓和错误均保留。 §27接通领会、观照和合宜结束的当下内容；一般Frame的已实现相关不要求额外未来产出，原接纳／在场／休息结果保留读法的不同内容。 §28接入既有共同操作因子与历史界面：完整接合说明须保留共同结构，却可省略问题范围外的内部事件。 §29新增三项纯核心适配，忠实归并在同一R上保留和恢复主张与步骤对应；模块11项空公理审计通过。 §30接用原反事实生成：替代行动及其他部分回应可由共同律产生，静态说明保留这些关系时具有同等地位。 §31以既有认识论证修正共同根据的读法：有根据的接受可错，实际承接的支持不等于保证结论为真。 选择经选择者的模型，不经记录：模型是备择的映射，两个备择被每个经它的选择选得相同，恰当它使二者并列（`Relabel.selected_alike_iff`）；模型是一个归并，经它的选择对要紧者正确，只在要紧者跨这一归并同去之处；模型归并了要紧者所分开的两个备择时，经它的选择都不正确，要分开它们需要更细的模型（`Learning.selects_right_through`、`Learning.selects_not_right_of_parted`）。记得更多并不提供哪个模型正确。 | Wisdom coordinates causal understanding, warranted concern and feasible response while revising facts, exclusion rules and power that blocks correction. A contextual continuation shows opposite responses under one requirement, including a reversal caused by the previous response’s success. A new candidate explains derivational productivity through traceable consequences of shared generative constraints; practical reasons acquire content through actual realization relations and explicit normative grounds. A subsequent response-necessity test shows distinct adequate descriptions of one process need not have an adequate common coarsening; a common root does not presuppose one least shared representation. A goal-evaluation continuation identifies omitted protection relations: identical old/new attainment profiles can have opposed practical frontiers, while a fixed relation-sensitive rule remains adequate. A limited-judgment continuation shows that a true risk statement permits actual failure; necessity tests must preserve claim content and strength, and a single outcome does not alone identify the prior bound. The whole-root coverage audit distinguishes episodes, formed capacities and full explanation: a common root must explain differentiated wisdom and its connections, without requiring every activity to enact every branch. A substantive comparison distinguishes support, applicability, recognition and realization roles of reasons. Particular constraints do not alone establish a universal basis; the common relations warranting transfer must be identified. Since morality weighs what follows from a change, it is read as a judgment of becoming, and since the theory’s model can judge becoming, as appraised through its universal generative principles (a reading, both premises not argued here); that those principles suffice for it is not shown here, and the ethics’ scope is the continuation condition’s and its order C’s, neither asserted (07 §4). Varied responses and tacit judgment do not alone defeat common normative grounds. Section 15 develops eight judgments and reversal conditions under fixed G0/J/E: common principles support opposed responses, preserve nonunique choice and locate omissions in judgment. The 13 October continuation reuses P75’s completed formal support: independently described judgment content, grounds, scope and strength are connected to common-response, generalization and bounded-identification conditions, separating theoretical understanding, tacit skill, epistemic scope, collective judgment and moral appraisal, and distinguishing warranted omission from harmful loss. Section 17 compares a concrete nonrepresentational skill account: explicit rules may be omitted while operative relations still require explanation; the existing constant-description boundary requires fixing the question’s content before evaluating preservation. Section 18 connects letting-go and ending: changed shared support and changed attention/experience are distinguished, while necessary relations allow old forms to end. Transfer, discharge and P91 fidelity prevent omitted needs being counted as improvement. Section 19 connects theoretical wisdom through content validity, grounds and actual understanding, separating common relations from branch conditions; existing closure/joint-interpretation results locate the universal burden at joint warrant for the root and rules. Section 20 separates operative grasp from discovery: chance discovery may precede understanding, direct apprehension need not be explicit inference, and redundant grounds can operate jointly. Existing acquired binding gives one route from content acquisition into interpretation and execution. Section 21 uses inherited substitution and equal-value capture to locate content dependence: necessary retention is relative to the attributed achievement, not the entire representation; core composition constrains continuation through one intermediate configuration. Section 22 audits whole-core interfaces: realization and supplied support/exchange have distinct completed preservation proofs. Achievement content must be connected through warranted correspondence, not representability or a shared dependency label. Section 23 reuses rather than rebuilds, since a connection already given within the one theory needs no parallel model: four pure adapters directly compose P91 admission fidelity and P93 selection preservation, with an exact criterion and inherited loss witness. P99 acquired revision/use and P89 realization/positive relevance are consumed as completed connections. Section 24 adds four adapters from P93 to arbitrary Frame realization, without numerical needs or decidability; a fixed-literal reading of the same supplied process separates selections across configurations from fidelity among claims at one configuration. Section 25 connects constitutive binding, operative acquisition and P103 supported continuation: the same run continues specified tasks while genuinely retiring an old distinction; content warrant, actual use and later support differentiate within the common activity. Section 26 connects existing fallible discrimination and timely-response necessity: a capacity with coverage and error commitments still needs adequate differences to enter use, allowing different internal descriptions, abstention and error. Section 27 connects present content in apprehension, contemplation and appropriate ending. General-Frame realized relevance requires no extra productive sequel; original admission/presence/rest results retain the readings’ different contents. Section 28 joins existing common operational factors and history interfaces: exact joining accounts retain common structure while internal events outside the question scope can remain omitted. Section 29 adds three pure-core adapters preserving and reflecting joint claim/step matching under faithful regrouping; all 11 module declaration audits are empty. Section 30 consumes existing counterfactual generation: a shared law generates the courses of supplied alternative acts and their responses; static accounts retaining these relations stand equally. Section 31 uses existing epistemic arguments to correct the common-ground reading: warranted acceptance can err, and operative support need not guarantee truth. Selection works through the selector's model, not the record: with the model a map of the alternatives, two alternatives are selected alike by every selection through it exactly when it ties them (`Relabel.selected_alike_iff`). The model is a lumping, and a selection through it is right about what matters only where what matters goes alike across the lumping; where the model lumps two alternatives that what matters separates, no selection through it is right, and separating them takes a finer model (`Learning.selects_right_through`, `Learning.selects_not_right_of_parted`).
