| M16 | 有效纠正的形成与战略脆弱性<br>Formation and strategic vulnerability of effective correction | 能够建设纠正能力与能够承受对方对其后续条件的破坏，是不同要求。 | Being able to build corrective capacity differs from being able to withstand attacks on its future conditions. | 条件战略构造与精确枚举已完成；保持历史验证范围，不升级为普遍制度结论。<br>Conditional strategic construction and exact enumeration completed; historical validation scope retained without a universal institutional conclusion. | [有效纠正的形成与战略脆弱性 / Formation and strategic vulnerability of effective correction](../history/FORMATION.md) |
| M17 | 关切、有效能力与更新的共同形成<br>Joint formation of concern, effective power and renewal | 学习、关切、查验、保护与后继重建可以在同一受支持的选择过程中相互接续。 | Learning, concern, inspection, protection and successor reconstruction can connect within one supported choice process. | 条件联合构造完成；廉价隔离、俘获和资源不足仍构成失败区，未证自然必然向善。<br>Conditional joint construction completed; cheap insulation, capture and inadequate resources remain failure regimes, with no inevitable favorable direction proved. | [关切、有效能力与更新的共同形成 / Joint formation of concern, effective power and renewal](../history/JOINT-FORMATION.md) |
| M18 | 可修订关切的联合实现<br>Joint realization of revisable concern | 理由修订、有效保护和资源支持的后继重建能够共存，但伦理过滤与战略均衡不能自动拼接。 | Reason revision, effective protection and supported successor reconstruction can coexist, but ethical filtering and strategic equilibrium do not compose automatically. | 条件联合过程与部分有限决策已建立；伦理锚点、审计可靠性和权限仍明确。<br>Conditional joint process and some finite decisions established; ethical anchors, audit soundness and authority remain explicit. | [可修订关切的联合实现 / Joint realization of revisable concern](../history/REVISABLE-CONCERN-SETTLEMENT.md) |
| M19 | 证据进入制度规则与实际补救<br>Evidence entering institutional rules and delivered remedy | 纠正个案不等于撤回失效的共同推断；纠正须沿实际依存传到规则、公共信息与补救。 | Correcting a case does not withdraw a defeated shared inference; correction must follow actual dependencies into rules, public information and remedy. | 有原始资料约束的历史应用与诊断契约；未估计设计的因果收益或完成普遍治理。<br>Primary-source-constrained historical application and diagnostic contract; no causal benefit estimate or complete general governance solution. | [证据进入制度规则与实际补救 / Evidence entering institutional rules and delivered remedy](../history/EVIDENCE-AND-INSTITUTIONAL-CORRECTION.md) |
| M20 | 生成性对齐<br>Generative alignment | 以生生为共同根据评价目标、评价者与选择规则；方向论证及相连目标取得、判断、实际修订与有界改革现已成为完成成果，更强理论按明确范围继续。 后续接通改革自身改变实际负担及报告路线、直接取得与保留新目的的修复。 理论接续区分发现已有根据、形成新生活与理由、修订评价承诺；学习后的赞同不独自认证形成过程。 在已完成的伦理学习基础上，新增比较接口辨明理由内容扩展后旧判断的传递、不可比及有前提的反转。 评价扩展进一步区分共同改善与各应用分别否定；旧内容保持不要求旧评价永久保持。 理论接续给出生活实现、支承与进一步形成的具体根据；共同生活不能以组织规模或产出代替。 后天形成的支承可以因其在基本生活中的实际作用取得保护；生成的评价须包括其形成的后续支持要求。 形成出的生活、承受和参与可以改变原服务角色的伦理根据；考虑、责任与支配权限分列。 新增取得记录到路线必要性分类的连接：忠实读取与选项覆盖支持有范围的判断，一个真实替代即可推翻不可替代性；缩小有根据解释与扩大可行选项分列。 自我评估的选择现在有精确覆盖条件：允许有根据的压缩，区分当前菜单与任意菜单；有限成功不能独自担保新范围，全域结构加辨别记录提供正面出路。 理论接续说明理解怎样实际参与目的和准备：关切可以与报酬及其他正当动机并存，其形成条件也须接受同一规范评价。 BC1 在同等基本救助的既定范围内支持保全更多生活；BC2 的一对一说明以可避免的实际毁损给出比较理由，区分境况、保护与时机，并保留机会争议。 形成中的评估区分当前能力、过去能力及形成方法。恢复过去的历史与取得新根据是两件事，所以历史区别无法恢复，不阻止以未来事件取得同一内容的新根据或修订理解；情境把握与风险估计可支持继续生成。 实际调查现接入描述与读取的共同修订：同一事件在某项估计中排除，不抹去其他有根据的表达意义；纠正归属与保留参与、支持可以属于同一回应。 关切发展接续具体化真实根据保留、改变及新形成时的双向纠正，并将学习中取得的解释纳入形成账户；五条候选路线保留各自正面作用。 后续路线评估以已有局部关切取得新关系内容并重组目的为理论主线，配套形成史判别，再选择机制研究；认识进步与实际新理由不以价值权重增加为定义。 LC 接通数量与程度共同改善的有限损失比较理由：实际损失的有根据匹配及额外保护可以共同支持方向；重复描述不增加生活，共同依存须进入损失判断。 情境风险接续区分个别改善与共同失效；精确有限界限允许部分把握和正风险，支持关系修订可解除共同中断，同时保留分散损失。 有限合并接续区分抵偿限制与理由删除：最强层过滤可在增加实际基本保护后翻转选择；固定比较与局部胜出拼接的条件分别公开。 关切内容形成现有一条具体候选：已有局部关切使粗略成功与实际回应的分离取得实践意义，关系辨别通过计划修订改变目的；实际新生活及对它的动机取得分列。 LR 为同等多救的充分优先提出局部稳定承诺，使一部分稍不等的基本损失有正面比较；新增共同保护另可逐次保留旧保护，增加原先冲突的实现。 同等基本风险分担RS现有正面理由：完整损失分布相当时，减轻无根据的暴露集中；同频率选择可产生不同真实风险，新信息也能支持进一步改规则。 现实市场与互联网为 enshittification 的机制追踪提供事件：以既有外部性解释为起点，查优势怎样改变未来选择及纠正条件，不将增长与损失并存当作新发现。 机会修订接续表明：最终保护的完整联合分布相同仍可能撤销正当安顿；选择规则须保留仍有效的历史要求，并说明真实纠正的根据。 陌生与无回报的关切扩展现有两段条件性说明：辨明对方在自身参与之外的生活，再取得陌生处境中的相关根据；共同史是形成入口，不是全部关切的边界。 CP为不等且未被充分排除的基本请求提出真实正机会要求，将共同分配权限与基本回应关系相连；它是新增且可争辩的进入承诺。 陌生生活辨认现接通具体实现、支撑关系、可修订证据与有限回应；观察与分类方式也可因遗漏的生活根据而修订。 共同接口史进一步支持直接经营控制与有偿影响的区分：反馈和替代的出现也可能进入维持优势的规则调整，改革需追踪实际控制关系。 RSL进一步提出有根据分担判断的有限延续；中间混合方案降低极端取舍，修订须携带理由及其整体适用范围。 风险接续新增 NP 候选：相同基本要求下，微小暴露差距不自动获得固定额外优先；在真实细分可行范围内，有条件地反对有损的精确均衡。 初次共同接管的机会限制现可连接到J3：先明确仍有效承担所保护的具体支持，再检验新安排是否兑现；名义正机会不独自完成接管任务。 选择压力下的关切现有个人补救—依存取得—共同支持—实际评定—接替的条件性形成说明；旧分数、真实损失及行动条件分别辨明。 退化边界续研区分伦理损失、返回机制与状态域：原市场模型中纠正余量精确决定统一门槛保护及指定低区证书的存在，同参数仍可保留良性域。 取得权力后的关切现有根据—有限授权—有内容的接替—可实行复核的条件性说明；充分比较可要求改变或结束原掌权方式。 随后新增实际Lean连接：具体强维护的逐情形覆盖导出同律保护质量下界；全非负自然数权重的保持刻画覆盖，一项实际法则同成功率仍可失履行。 情境把握新增有条件的估计—保留误差界—行动根据连接：变化中可收敛或有界追踪，具有正比较余量时可在有限阶段取得充分认证。 纠正入口现有由共同准备、有限授权与实际承接形成的有限候选；正当回应可跨过不愿纠正的协调者，动机取得与回应实现分别说明。 风险条件接续要求同时交代当前支持的概率对应与仍有效历史；固定旧分数或不断重算分数都不足以完成规则对齐。 指定维护新增实际执行组合：已有观察分支选择器取得真实读数并实施，经明确任务效果对应导出履行及质量下界；有限预算中旧维护仍可行而完整扩展失败，增资后付费读取或实际并行均能扩展保护。 取得的行动响应可进入预测，补偿持续变化；精度须与行动幅度共同判断，有限相关条件可联合达到足够认证，扩展前沿仍可有新未明关系。 §§75–77在当时所取的 G0 路线之下先以整体生成之善评价组织，不先设对权力的反对，因为以首次授权为门槛，会把局部机制的权限前提抬成全理论的先决裁判：组织没有先验的收缩义务，权力多少不能代替这一比较；协调和行动能力的扩大可以是正面成果，扩大、维持或收缩均无预设优先。所以旧授权之墙的基础地位已撤回。 局部吸引续研已把原市场模型的双吸引与内部不稳定接回同一历史的实际支持：选择组织能够吸收扰动，同时耗尽必要支持；高端保护与低端有限失效分列。 诊断读数取得校正和相互作用关系，实际接入预测；有限早期错误后，正确修复可恢复收敛，持续有界残差支持最终误差范围。 维护参照新增对应纠错：接管自身撤掉支持后，用受损能力重算目标可使当前证书空过关而原任务失履行；同任务真实完成或仍待覆盖的内容可以共同接续原目标，完成有限交付后可以退役。 六篇原始实验现已接入：情境学习、描述修订及有效行动恢复已有直接经验支持，操作性说明的文献实证整合完成。 一元关系接续新增共同能力→C的实际证明组合：各项分别可能性相同仍可有共同生成的严格差别；只依赖这些单项信息的严格评价不能承载该比较。 一元组织论证现从完整改善见证推出组织形成要求，可支持建设与增强并保留承载选择；建设实绩与整体生成判断分列。 正面目标随后在当时所取的 G0 路线之下得到纠正：正面根据不必等待对整体的完备排序；同一回应若在当前有根据的每条接续中都形成某种新的生成内容，它便有正面根据，而无需预定同一种形态。有限贡献与已取得证据接通；真实未来分支和进一步辨别分别保留。 已复算16人的2,656次公开恢复测量，并评估800次留后预测，形成实际数据对应。 有支持的有限纠正已接通原保护区：任意正维护余量可在实际阻断侵蚀期间有限到达，逐步支持界允许撤除后沿原规律继续。支持覆盖是一般持续律的梯级族（起点在每个可容许扰动下持续n步，恰当某个梯级族在第n级含有它），在最坏份额下恰好。 开放形态下的规则修订现有正式实现组合：当前路线推进与剩余证书交接接入既有有限更新，再在每次完成对应新的活的内容时实现这种内容，它在延续条件之下落入由意义读出的相关性（07 §3.2）；各自会完成的路线反复切换仍可能永久不完成。 分类稳健比较现已证明：所比较的实现已判定时，新内容全部列一般与全部列基本这两个端点同向严格改善，足以覆盖所有混合分类，并可接成各分类下的组织形成要求；作用要求不保证共同最优方案。 部分纠正进一步取得精确到达边界：0≤β≤3/4时，原无误差模型所有正维护初态均有限进入保护区，当且仅当β≤5/12，这是一般到达律的实例（起点在每个可容许扰动下n步内到达目标，恰当某个漏斗族在第n级含有它），市场的增益估计（依次为份额、影响、质量）给出漏斗族；临界点中实际余量能改善后续影响。 有限支持边界进一步明确：任意有限C₀≥5均有正维护余量区间，在原扩展与耗用关系下对全部时变责任纠正仍会先失效，这是耗竭层律的实例（低于第n级的起点在每段可容许历史上n步内离开守卫）；同一正初态投影有限恢复而完整历史终止。 24人更新数据进一步支持形成后的选择性变化，扰动方向效应增强较明确。 实际形成接口补入LibreOffice历史：原有工作、新发布流程、过渡承担与Ubuntu分发先于新基金会法人完成；部分改组可以先支撑下一段活动。 同一软件接续评估加入Narwhal：未完成改造、继续接入计划与后续发布分别记录；未来API改价顾虑进入月度承诺安排，运行适应不独自证明后续改组控制已变。 P89补齐开放形态成果在共同 Frame／Run 与意义定义上的正式连接；旧输出接入相关性接口，使得在每次完成都对应新的活的内容之处，所形成的内容在延续条件之下落入由意义读出的相关性，目的修订及在途工作成果按既有范围直接复用。 P91 已补比较内容的分组保真：同一过程不变，依存可判定时，同组最低所需逐项相同恰好保证所有水平上的接纳内容可恢复；局部保真将原扩展比较保留到分组后。 及时接续的应用论证将支承的理由接到实际需要、过渡与共同替代：最终回程或焦点存续不能独自认证修复；可形成新共同能力而不要求旧载体永存。 FR 候选进一步反对同类可恢复基本损失中的永久程度优先：在支持、负担与历史条件保留的规模扩展中，固定的较轻基本保护可于有限规模取得充分选择根据。 P100 将完整活动片段接到同一修复过程：明确匹配充分性成立时，对同一生活的两次阻断，别处一次保护不足、三次保护足够，这些是损失匹配计数律的实例（同属一类时，严格损失匹配存在，恰当较好的配置失去的列出主张更少）；历史损失不被当前支持恢复抹去。 P102 完成完整片段的适用资格：区分叙述切分、损失分化与比较可替换，保留跨段后效和共同背景。持续损伤直接按整段修复及有根据参照比较；不足端与充分端分别取得，不要求先有统一时长价格。 | Appraise ends, evaluators and selection rules through shared generative grounds; the direction argument and connected acquisition, judgment, operative revision and bounded reform are now completed foundations for the broader programme. A further joined case connects reform-induced burdens and reporting changes to acquired correction and repair preserving the new goal. The theoretical continuation distinguishes discovering existing grounds, forming new living relations and reasons, and revising normative commitments; later endorsement alone does not authorize the formation process. Building on the completed ethical-learning account, a new comparison interface delimits preservation, incomparability and conditional reversal after warranted content extension. Evaluative extension further separates a common improvement from defeat in each live application; retained old content need not preserve every old verdict. The continuation articulates living realization, support and further formation as particular grounds; organizational scale and output cannot substitute for the shared living being assessed. Acquired support can warrant basic protection through its actual role in living; appraisal of formation includes the subsequent support requirements it creates. Formed living, undergoing and participation can change the grounds of an original service role; consideration, responsibility and governing authority remain distinct. A further connection derives scoped route classification from faithful acquired records and covered options; one genuine alternative defeats indispensability. Narrowing supported interpretations and expanding eligible options are distinct changes. Self-review selection now has exact certificate-coverage conditions: warranted compression is allowed, current-menu and every-menu adequacy differ, and finite success alone cannot certify a new scope. Whole-domain structure plus discriminating records supplies a positive route. The conceptual continuation explains how understanding can participate in purposes and preparation: concern can coexist with pay and other legitimate motives, while its formative conditions face the same normative appraisal. BC1 favors more extensive equivalent basic rescue in its established scope. The one-to-one account of BC2 supplies a reason based on preventable impairment, distinguishing baseline, protection and timing while retaining fair-chance disputes. Formative assessment distinguishes current capacity, earlier capacity and the method of formation. Recovering an earlier history and acquiring fresh grounds are different things, so unavailable history need not prevent fresh grounds for the same content or revised understanding through future events; situational awareness and risk estimates can support continued generativity. Actual inquiry now connects joint description-and-reading revision to practice: excluding an event from one estimate need not erase its independently warranted expressive significance. Corrected attribution and continued participation/support can belong to one response. The developmental continuation specifies bidirectional correction as living grounds persist, change or form, and includes acquired interpretation during learning; five candidate routes retain their positive roles. A subsequent route assessment prioritizes acquired relational content and purpose revision within partial concern, with discriminating formation histories and conditional mechanistic investigation; epistemic progress and new particular reasons are not defined by increased valuation weights. LC supplies a limited comparative reason where number and gravity jointly improve: warranted matching and additional actual protection can support a direction together. Duplicate descriptions do not multiply living; shared dependence must inform loss assessment. Situated risk now distinguishes marginal improvement from shared failure. Exact finite bounds permit partial awareness and nonzero risk; revising support can remove joint interruption while retaining dispersed losses. The aggregation continuation distinguishes limited counterbalancing from deletion of reasons: a strongest-tier filter reverses under added actual protection; fixed-comparison and local-composition premises are separately exposed. A concrete concern-content candidate links a coarse success conception, meaningful-response mismatch, acquired relational distinctions and operative purpose revision; actual new shared goods and their motivational uptake remain distinct. LR adds a local stability commitment to BC1’s sufficient priority, supporting some slightly unequal basic-loss comparisons. Separately, newly feasible joint protection can preserve old protection on each realization and add previously conflicting protection. RS now supplies a defended reason to share comparable basic risk while preserving the complete loss profile. Equal selection frequencies can conceal different exposure; new information can support further rule revision. Existing market and internet histories provide events for tracing enshittification: start with established externality explanations and investigate how advantage changes future selection and correction, rather than treating growth alongside loss as a new finding. The chance-revision continuation shows that even equal full terminal joint laws can conceal replacement of a warranted settlement; selectors must preserve live historical requirements and explain genuine correction. A conditional extension account now distinguishes continuation beyond one's participation from acquired grounds in unfamiliar lives; shared history can form concern without delimiting every ground it can address. CP proposes actual positive opportunity for unequal basic requests not adequately excluded, grounding a limit on common allocative authority in the response relationship; it is an additional contestable admission commitment. An unfamiliar-life recognition account now connects particular realization, support, revisable evidence and bounded response; omitted living grounds can also warrant revision of observation and classification. A shared-interface history supports distinguishing direct operational control from purchased influence: feedback and alternatives can enter rule changes that preserve advantage, so reforms must track actual control relations. RSL proposes limited retention of a warranted sharing judgment. Intermediate mixtures refine the choice; revision must carry its grounds and their whole-comparison scope. Risk continuation adds NP: tiny exposure differences do not automatically confer a fixed extra priority among matched basic claims. It conditionally rejects costly exact equality when sufficiently fine departures are feasible. Initial common takeover constraints are now connected to J3: specify the concrete support still due, then test whether the replacement fulfills it. Nominal positive opportunity alone does not discharge the task. A conditional account now connects personal compensation, acquired dependencies, shared support, consequential assessment and succession under selection pressure, distinguishing score loss, living loss and conditions for action. The degeneration extension separates ethical loss, returning selection and state domains: an exact correction margin governs uniform floor protection and existence of the specified low-region certificates, while a favorable region can coexist. A conditional account connects living grounds, bounded authorization, meaningful succession and effective review; the inherited sufficient comparison can require changing or ending incumbent control. A subsequent checked Lean bridge derives common-law support mass bounds from pointwise maintenance; preservation for all nonnegative natural weights characterizes coverage, while equal success under one actual law can still miss the undertaking. Situated awareness now has a conditional estimation–retained uncertainty–decision evidence connection: convergence or bounded tracking through change, with eventual certification under a strict comparison margin. A finite candidate forms corrective access through cooperative preparation, bounded delegation and actual succession; warranted response can pass an unwilling coordinator, with motive acquisition and performance kept distinct. Risk conditioning requires both current-support probability correspondence and warranted continuing history; neither freezing nor repeatedly recalculating a score completes rule alignment. Specified maintenance now has an actual-execution composition: the existing contingent selector acquires an observation and responds, with explicit task-effect correspondence yielding fulfillment and a mass bound. Old maintenance remains feasible when full expansion fails; funded sensing or real parallel provision realizes the extension. Acquired action response can enter prediction and compensate sustained change. Precision must be assessed with action magnitude; a finite relevant family can jointly reach adequate certification while an expanding frontier retains new unknowns. §§75–77 appraise organization through whole generative goodness, under the G0 route then taken, before any presumption against power, since a threshold of first authorization would raise the authority premises of local mechanisms into a prior judge of the whole theory: institutions have no a priori duty to contract, and more power or less power cannot replace the comparison. Expanded coordination and action can be positive achievements; expansion, maintenance and contraction have no preset ranking. So the former foundational authority wall is withdrawn. The attraction continuation connects two local attractors and interior instability to actual support in the same market history: a selection organization can absorb perturbations while exhausting essential support; high-side protection and low-side finite failure remain distinct. Diagnostic readings yield corrections and an interaction relation that enter prediction. After finite earlier errors, exact repair restores convergence; persistent bounded residual supports eventual containment. Maintenance reference now has an intervention-correspondence diagnostic: withdrawing support can empty a recomputed current target while leaving the original undertaking unfulfilled. Actual completion of the same task or retained current requirements can transport the original content; completed finite delivery permits retirement. Six primary experimental studies are now integrated: situated learning, revision and useful recovery have direct empirical support, completing the targeted operational evidence synthesis. A new relational composition connects joint capacity to C: identical marginal possibilities can conceal a strict joint-generative difference, so marginal-only strict appraisal cannot implement that comparison. The monist organizational argument now derives a formation requirement from whole improvement witnesses, supporting constructive capacity with carrier latitude and distinguishing construction success from whole generative appraisal. The positive target is then corrected, under the G0 route then taken: a positive ground need not wait on a complete ranking of the whole; where every supported visible continuation of one response forms some new generative content, that response has a positive ground without fixing one common form. Finite contribution and acquired evidence connect while real branching and further discrimination remain distinct. Reanalysis of 2,656 public recovery measurements from 16 people and 800 held-out predictions supplies actual data correspondence. Supported finite correction now reaches the original protection region from every positive renewal seed under effective suppression; a prefix support bound permits continuation under the original law after withdrawal. The support cover is a ladder of the general lasting law (a start lasts n steps under every admissible disturbance exactly when some ladder holds it at level n), exact at the worst share. Open-form rule revision now has a checked realization composition: current-rule progress and remaining-certificate handoff feed existing finite renewal, then, where each completion corresponds to new living content, the run realizes such content, which under the continuation condition falls under the relevance read from significance (07 §3.2). Individually finishing rules can still fail forever under repeated switching. Classification-robust comparison is now proved: where the realizations compared are decided, strict agreement at the all-secondary and all-basic endpoints covers every mixed assignment and can require formation under each classification; a required role does not ensure one common undefeated implementation. Partial correction yields an exact arrival boundary: for 0 ≤ beta ≤ 3/4, all positive renewal states in the original zero-error model reach protection in finite time iff beta<=5/12, an instance of the general reaching law (a start reaches a target within n steps under every admissible disturbance exactly when some funnel holds it at level n), the market's gain estimates (share, then influence, then quality) supplying the funnels; retained activity improves subsequent influence at equality. Finite support adds a quantified boundary: every finite C0>=5 has a positive renewal interval that fails under all time-varying quality corrections with the inherited growth and stock laws, an instance of the law of drained levels (a start below level n leaves the guard within n steps on every admissible history); the same seed has projected finite recovery but full-history termination. Updating data from 24 people further support selective formed changes, with clearer growth in the perturbation contrast. The LibreOffice history adds an actual formation interface: inherited work, a new release process, interim support and Ubuntu distribution precede foundation incorporation; partial reorganization can support further activity. The same software-continuation assessment adds Narwhal: unfinished change, continued-access plans and a later release are distinguished; concern about future API repricing enters monthly commitments, so operational adaptation does not establish changed control over later adaptation. P89 completes the formal open-form connection on shared Frame/Run and significance definitions: the inherited output feeds the relevance interface, so that, where each completion corresponds to new living content, the content formed falls under the relevance read from significance under the continuation condition, while existing goal-revision and pending-work results are reused at their checked scopes. P91 completes comparison-content fidelity under grouping: on one unchanged process, decidable requirements give exact all-level recovery iff group members have identical minimum profiles; local fidelity preserves the existing extension comparison. The timely-continuation application connects support warrant to actual needs, transition and joint replacement: eventual return or focal persistence alone does not certify repair; new common capacities can form without perpetuating old carriers. The FR candidate further opposes permanent gravity priority within same-kind recoverable basic losses: with support, burdens and historical conditions retained under scale expansion, fixed lesser basic protections can become sufficient for choice at a finite scale. P100 connects complete activity episodes to one repair process: under explicit matching sufficiency, one protection elsewhere is insufficient against two interruptions of the same life, while three suffice, instances of the counting law of loss matching (within one content class, a strict loss match exists exactly when the better configuration loses fewer listed claims); restored current support does not erase historical loss. P102 completes the qualification of whole episodes: distinguish narrative partition, differentiated loss and comparative substitutability while retaining cross-episode effects and common context. Sustained harm is compared through whole-course repair and warranted references, obtaining insufficient and sufficient anchors separately without a universal duration price. | 2026-10-08 No full basin, all-parameter bifurcation, real-state extension, calibration or independent acceptance. Adds 17 Lean theorems / 21 audits, with 32 inherited theorems / 41 audits separate. Common reference, bilinear family, access permission and error/transfer bounds remain explicit; offset confounding and curvature missed by all four probes remain checked counterexamples. No new empirical validation. Four new Lean theorems / 13 audits pass, with 21 imported theorems / 55 audits separate, 29 artifacts, three extra examples and independent source/receipt acceptance. One ambiguous correspondence paragraph is corrected with original text and both hashes retained; old theorems are unchanged. A past single delivery is not continuing capacity. Task warrant, true records and grounds of revision remain explicit; no empirical validation is added. No new theorem, experiment or raw-data reanalysis. Reading scope and adverse cases are retained study by study. Complementary experiments support mechanism connections without calibrating this observer or establishing full biological joint validation or inevitable ethical convergence. This addition has 6 new Lean theorems / 12 empty new audits; 16 reused theorems / 48 audits are separate. Independent source/receipt review accepts the scoped composition. Living meaning, complete loss coverage and C applicability remain explicit; joint possibility guarantees neither actualization nor complete ethical quality. Thirteen new Lean theorem declarations/29 audits and 30 inherited theorems/46 audits remain separate. Costs, opportunity loss and overbuilding survive; the local report omission is repaired. Author counterreview, no empirical or independent acceptance. Nine new theorems / thirty audits pass with independent review; seventy-two inherited theorems / 133 audits remain separate. G0, correspondence and direct contributory articulation are explicit. C, global losslessness, full integration and omniscience are not gates. The retained query field is not a newly executed channel; the adverse-total example is not an action recommendation. Finite retention, individual variation, uncertain group differences, simpler forecast winners and processing discrepancies remain; no new experiment or theorem. This continuation checks 27 new theorems / 35 audits and separately 96 inherited theorems / 118 audits; zero seed, no uniform pulse duration and prior support failure remain, with actual suppression and entry still to be explained. This composition checks 10 new Lean theorems / 19 audits, separately from 98 inherited theorems / 185 audits. Certificates are not ethical quantities; events 0..B represent at most B+1 actual transitions. Variable form does not license arbitrary replacement of undertakings or impose bounded completion on all life or inquiry. This round checks 13 new Lean theorems/25 audits and separately 73 inherited theorems/144 audits. Fixed content correspondence and G0/J remain explicit; actual opportunity loss and the three-route no-common-implementation boundary remain. Author review, with no new empirical or independent acceptance. Twenty-six new theorems / 31 audits are separate from 123 inherited theorems / 153 audits; supercritical nonarrival boxes and critical positive-state obstructions under arbitrarily small constant adverse errors remain. Finite-support obstruction adds 17 Lean theorems / 22 audits, with 123 inherited theorems / 153 audits separate; delta_N is a conservative failure interval, excluding new provision, dependence change and entry, without universal phase or zero-substantive-axiom claims. Acquired cue-effect growth remains uncertain and its later effect is weaker; current differences, formation-related changes and persistence stay distinct. Seven primary statements and preservation checks with author counterreview; restricted capital and operating support are distinguished, and Apache’s other continuation retained. No new mathematics, complete financial trajectory or numerical market adapter; no universal reorganization guarantee. Five newly examined developer posts and two reread statements; currently retrieved historical pages may be edited and provide neither prospective prediction, matched causal identification nor complete contracts. Apollo’s ending and Narwhal’s continuation remain; the rule-influence return loop is still missing. No new result identity or mathematics. P89 adds four checked Lean adapters with empty axiom reports to the existing result, not four discoveries. Decided completion, execution, handoff, generative correspondence and Unrestricted remain explicit. Generic bounded realization and the formal connection are closed; NP strength, mixed losses and stronger normative warrant remain unresolved, without reopening completed generic goal-revision mechanisms. P91: 24 new Lean theorems/33 axiom-free declaration audits, with core and peripheral compatibility checks passed. Two feasible courses generated from one start and rest-law have real opposed gains which an unfaithful grouping hides. Identity interchangeability, joint feasibility, complete mixed ordering and full axiom-free warrant do not follow. P95 reuses checked return, substitution and handoff results as interpretive application with author counterreview, adding no mathematics or independent result; it keeps the G0/J conditional interface of that time, G0 then folded into significance, with J2 strength and crossed basic losses explicit. P96 adds a normative candidate beyond LR’s neighborhood with author counterreview, no new mathematics or independent acceptance; saturating and chance-based rivals, actual scale conditions, uncalibrated sufficiency boundaries and opportunity cost remain explicit. P100: 17 new Lean theorems and 45 source declarations have empty axiom reports, reusing shared Frame/Run and earlier priority transport. Complete-episode correspondence and sufficiency remain explicit; available support is not full experienced recovery. Two against two gives only a loss correspondence; backbone failure defeats the match. No general FR, arbitrary-H calibration or new independent acceptance. P102 is philosophical correspondence and author counterreview, adding no mathematics, axiom, empirical certification or independent acceptance; it reuses rejection/improvement transport. Restoration is neither necessary for every decomposition nor sufficient for equal counting. Filling P100’s recovery gap does not automatically preserve three-protection sufficiency. Read with the direction of 生生, consciousness differentiates living significance, and new living can count positively, as a reason under living significance with a supplied articulation and correspondence (07 §3.2) and in a ranking of courses under C or a premise in its place (07 §4), without assigning a specific duty. Preservation can require renewal; the sharper dispute concerns new realization beyond incumbent claims and preservation targets. Redescription cannot erase destruction, and rejecting a welfare-based creation reason does not reject every generative ground. Conditional care does not independently exhaust formation reasons; excluding a further favorable ground needs its own defense. Whole-history mixed improvement and warranted reallocation yield favorable formation; stage-relative judgments need explicit transport. A complete ordering is not an additional gate for basic positive significance; favorable formation reasons and practical requirements retain distinct grounds. Living significance is not awarded for regulatory success or self-sufficiency; dependent living and the whole consequences of local success remain in view. The consciousness comparison closes the two questions: a welfare restriction alone does not exhaust ethical significance, and changed feeling requires reassessment without automatic retention or erasure of all value. The inquiry-purpose test distinguishes correctness, reasons to inquire and wider living grounds; understanding can itself be worth realizing, but using science alone does not refute a restriction of G0. The common-living-ground test shows how G0/J1/J4 can favor nonconscious living continuation under no-loss and legitimate-feasibility conditions; that conditional difference does not prove G0, nor the continuation condition or C, neither of which the theory asserts (07 §3–§4). The reason-authority test grants objective practical reasons while requiring an explanation of why their content reaches G0 living scope; purpose revision and fallible correction retain their positive roles. The completed reason-content comparison connects care, understanding, legitimate undertakings and further living; a common ground need not exhaust all value, and relational goods can matter for their own sake. The welfare-boundary test permits conscious welfare alongside wider basic living significance; comparative priority preserves foregone good, and the dispute concerns whether welfare exhausts basic ethical grounds. The welfare-exhaustion comparison distinguishes realized good, marginal improvement and duty; nonconscious living locates a substantive ground dispute, while rejecting one restriction does not uniquely establish G0 or every additional commitment. The affirmative flourishing test distinguishes biological performance, basic significance and whole choice: complete flourishing is not an admission threshold, and reproduction or typicality does not independently determine whole goodness. The restored target requires compatibility, without demanding a unique formulation or actual assent; explicit exclusion of basic living significance supplies the corresponding conflict. The practical-authority test reuses completed responsibility principles: changed inclination alone does not discharge a justified undertaking, and epistemic authority does not automatically establish practical authority. This continuation checks a necessary condition of fallible scientific discrimination: coverage is constrained by the two error weights and available distinctions, with adaptive correspondence across different internal states. The shared spine now supplies the necessity correspondence for testing within the couplings, generalization, bounded identification and conditional structural reconstruction; the complete test family and actual finite observations remain distinct. The fallible bound now connects to native common-law response: under balance, the same formations present, alike initial levels on what couples in beyond responsive reach, and complete readouts, coverage is bounded by two errors plus responsive-reach weight. Testing balance further separates correct answers from complete mechanism explanation: common unaccounted exchange suffices for beyond-reach blindness, absolute balance is a special case, and accounting for the residual still needs its response explained. P97 separates actual warrant from exhaustive device knowledge: justify the restrictions needed for a particular inference while permitting scoped abstraction and uncertainty. P101 fixes a stage-relative continuation task: preserve the subsequent operations executable there and their warrant; contextualized history accounts can qualify without requiring branching-graph notation. P104 connects exchange order to deadline discrimination: under common initial levels and stated mechanism conditions, commitment is bounded by paired error plus timely potential reach; future support cannot count as an earlier coupling. P105 connects scientific standing to warranted discrimination and operative correction, distinguishing truth, evidence quality and research stage; agreement with this theory does not define science. | | M23 | 因应转化的条件与方向<br>Conditions and direction of responsive transformation | 接受更新不独自保持实际纠正入口；维护有真实代价，过滤后还须重算接续价值。有限期组合保留前缀入口并达到域内最优。 后续有限权重反例表明：局部修复还须能依实际观察执行；精确最优策略仍可有1/16不实断言风险。 主动观察还可破坏先前成立的风险分离；保证须覆盖后续观察，并保持实际记录与命令送达。 双通道协议实际取得并更新解码记录，保留旧入口直到新的当前记录就绪；匹配版本不独自保证记录真实。 终端纠正可实现当且仅当同一观察下有共同成功命令；相反隐藏解码阻断保证，受保护使用区间给出条件修复。 多系统互补观察与分址作用可以改变该边界；共同整体不排除有用试验，但局部成功不独自支持联合干预。 准确可能性接口不独自保持可观察纠正；复合探测与关系修复可起作用，保持复合关系允许内部继续变化。 进一步区分完整端点关系与实际请求历史：逐段准备新路径，同时保护每个在途请求所引用的旧路径。 局部机制可以在其范围内保证某一效果，而这一范围未必在此后每次变化中都持续，所以所研究的是认知与处境变化之中的边界条件和方向；主词是因应转化（AI 所取的工作名），因为它不预设分开的纠正者与被纠正者；可纠正性保留为AI对齐读者桥接。 | Acceptance does not alone preserve correction access. Maintenance has a real cost, and filtering requires recomputed continuation values. Finite-horizon composition retains prefix access and attains the constrained optimum. A finite-weight counterexample shows that a local repair must be executable from available observations: exact optimality can retain a 1/16 false-assertion risk. Chosen sensing can invalidate an initially sufficient risk floor; guarantees must cover later observations and preserve usable records and command delivery. A two-channel protocol acquires and renews decoding records while retaining the old route until the new one is ready; matching versions alone do not establish record soundness. Terminal correction is realizable exactly when each observation admits a common successful command; conflicting hidden decoders obstruct guarantees, while a protected use interval supplies a conditional repair. Complementary observations and separately addressed actions across systems can change that boundary; a shared whole permits useful trials, but local success alone does not justify joint intervention. An exact possibility interface does not alone preserve observable correction; aggregate probes and relational repair can work, while preserving the composed relation allows continuing internal change. Complete endpoint relations are further distinguished from actual request histories: prepare a new route incrementally while protecting each in-flight request’s referenced route. A local mechanism may guarantee an effect within its range without that range persisting through every later change, so the question studied is conditions and direction through changing understanding and circumstances; the primary term is responsive transformation (an AI working name), since it presupposes no separate corrector and corrected object; corrigibility remains an AI-alignment reader bridge. | 原34项定理／56项审计及12,960项比较保留；新增31项定理／54项审计，含随机策略最优界、可执行修复界及风险分离条件。论文对应经作者反审，形成具体前沿贡献供外部核查；独立验收、绝对原创性及实际 AI 优势未证。 再新增28项定理／40项审计、432项计划；如实对照与真实代价保留。后续23项定理／45项审计实现原子协议中任意前缀及无界调度的有限时刻安全，指定交接完成；472个状态、3,796条转移及100次交接通过。真实探测、全部变化受管理与可用通道仍为前提，实际AI停机未证。 后续34项定理／58项审计、63,504个案例及60次线程试验：任意有限记录不能消除已允许的最终歧义，随机最坏成功率至多1/2。底层方法已有先例；一般部署对应及独立原创性是当前墙。 多系统接续新增37项定理／58项审计，19/31继承分列；实际传递、试验包含及共同变化范围为新的对应墙。 关系接续新增39/55：成对修订的任意有限历史保持原纠正，拆开实施有中途失败；实际协调与动态分布式实现仍待证。 版本接续新增33项定理／62项审计，继承62项定理／100项审计分列；4,512个状态、63,168条转移、100次交接及80次受控线程试验通过。保留原子取得与 pin 登记、生命周期及有限资源前提；既有网络更新获归属，实际AI对应与独立原创性未证。 本次为解释修订，无新数学结果；既有条件证明、处境变化反例及仍有根据的责任保留，不把无条件保证设为方向完成门槛。<br>Original 34/56 proof package and 12,960 comparisons retained. Followup: 31 theorems / 54 audits, including randomized-policy optimality, executable repair bounds and a risk-floor condition. Explicit paper correspondence and author counterreview yield a concrete contribution for external scrutiny; independent acceptance, absolute priority and learned-AI advantage remain open. Further extension: 28 theorems / 40 audits and 432 plans, retaining safe controls and costs. A further 23 theorems / 45 audits realize prefix safety and safety at every finite point of unbounded atomic schedules, plus prescribed handoff completion; 472 states, 3,796 transitions and 100 handoffs pass. Faithful probing, mediated changes and channel availability remain premises; actual AI shutdown is unproved. Further 34 theorems / 58 audits, 63,504 cases and 60 thread trials: no finite transcript removes the admitted terminal ambiguity; randomized worst-case success is at most 1/2. Underlying methods are established; general deployment correspondence and independent novelty are the current walls. Plural-system continuation adds 37 theorems / 58 audits, with inherited 19/31 separate; actual transfer, trial containment and joint variation define the new correspondence wall. Relational extension adds 39/55: every finite history of paired revisions preserves the retained correction, but splitting an update fails between stages; physical coordination and dynamic distributed implementation remain unproved. Versioned continuation adds 33 theorems / 62 audits, with inherited 62 theorems / 100 audits separate; 4,512 states, 63,168 transitions, 100 handoffs and 80 controlled thread trials pass. Atomic acquisition with pin registration, lifetime and finite-resource premises remain; established network updates retain credit, while actual AI correspondence and independent novelty remain open. This is an interpretive revision with no new mathematical result. Existing conditional proofs, changed-circumstance failures and warranted responsibilities remain; unconditional assurance is not the direction’s completion gate. | [复核时的对齐表述 / Alignment account as | M24 | 元吸引子与选择条件的重建<br>Meta-attractors and reproduction of selection conditions | 从同一活动的支承、留痕和接续说明共同组织及调节作用的形成。活动后果参与再生产选择该组织的条件，组织因而可经具体承载变化接续；它可以支承修订，也可以关闭修订。完整整合接通实际失配→构造／检查方法改变→新参考与候选→装置安装和真实回应→成功生产支承更替及再次修订，并区分严格吸引、恢复路径与滑入诊断。修订经存量的一般规律运行：重建与纠正的历程可执行，恰当关联过程容许且存量覆盖，二者单独都不执行它；断供时没有无尽运行，修订循环无尽重复，恰当更替的回补覆盖每轮用量。共同任务、局部服从造成的共同损失、目的修订和在途任务的条件成果保留，不默认利益对立、固定共同效用或监管不可靠。 | Common organization and regulation form through support, retained differences and continuation within one activity. Consequences help reproduce conditions selecting that organization across changing carriers; this can sustain or close revision. The completed integration joins actual mismatch, changed construction/checking methods, fresh references and candidates, installed apparatus and actual response, then productive support for succession and further revision. | M26 | 认知发展与取得的组合解释<br>Cognitive development and acquired compositional interpretation | 取得的组合操作进入沟通、分节、绑定及新使用；行动与访问记录改变后续经历。后得来源关系重释旧命名，日期化证据进一步约束其历史适用范围。经验覆盖与保留边界、错误确信及暂时变化失败保留，完整认知目标未完成。 发育与比较证据进一步区分访问关系的取得、保持及任务中的实际使用。 后果现将学习所得接入更新组织的重构：零矩阵的实际取得、后续表现、记录及更新控制的传递与后继使用接成同一段历史。在所述构造中，这对每次接触之后的忠实重构都成立：它传递网络与更新控制位，按容量建成后继，并保持材料与支持。整体表现可影响组成关系的接续；一般连接在条件范围内完成。 绑定结果现已成为对每类记录与历史成立的律：分组恢复每个编码序列的词，当且仅当每个词内部相连，且任何词的末元素都不连到任何词的首元素；意义可判定地比较时，形式在不重复的候选范围中解析为某意义，恰当每个关涉该形式的记录都提供它，且其他每个意义都缺于某个关涉的记录，后来的记录只缩小候选；报告与内容可判定地比较时，日期化记录在一族历史中确定旧记录的内容，恰当每个以另一内容给出同一记录的族内对手都被某一日期化控制区分。行动场景与合作命名是第二条律的实例，回溯重读是它经一族解释读记录的形式的实例，四时刻来源史是第三条律的实例；其失败（在一处命名、错时命名、后来的路线改变、遗漏的暂时变化）落在律不成立的一侧。 观察在下游之内：对每个读入每个条件一个所列水平之时程的律，同样形成在场、部分动作相同、其余遵从律而起始只在一个储量上不同的两个平衡进程，在每一步、在按到那一步形成的所读路线不在储量下游的每个条件上都相同（`apart_only_downstream`）；所以一个形成的交换只经它所读、位于储量下游的水平回应储量之差（`exchange_alike_off_downstream`），直接观察是长度为零的路线。运行分开之处，前段与律都不固定其去向：两个运行在一步分开，指它们经映射的历程在此步之前相同、在此步不同；此前的前段是同一个，所以没有外推，无论被给予什么（律在内），在两个运行上都对（`Parting.not_right_on_both`）；移动与配置列出、步已定时，依过程所建之外推在一步于每个运行上都对，恰当那一步没有两个运行分开（`Parting.world_right_iff`）。从一个配置出发没有两个运行分开、值已定、运行被 N 个配置的列表所持时，按最小界的外推不被给予什么，从 2N − 1 起都对（`Parting.leastBound_right`），同于确定的律，后者处处不分开（`Parting.unparted_of_determined`）。 | Acquired composition enters communication, grouping, binding and new use; activity and access records change later encounters. Later source learning reinterprets old naming, and dated evidence constrains its historical applicability. Empirical coverage/retention limits, mistaken confidence and temporary-change failure remain; the full cognitive goal is incomplete. Developmental and comparative evidence further distinguishes formation, retention and task-specific use of access relations. Consequences now connect learned use to reconstruction of the updating organization: actual zero-matrix acquisition, subsequent performance, copied record/procedure and successor use form one connected history. In the declared construction this holds of every exposure followed by a faithful reconstruction: it passes on the network and procedure bit, builds as many successors as capacity allows and conserves material and support. Whole performance can affect constituent continuation; the general connection is completed at its conditional scope. The binding results now hold as laws for every type of records and histories: grouping recovers the words of every encoded sequence exactly when each word is linked inside and no word's last element is linked to any word's first; with meanings compared decidedly, a form resolves to a meaning in a repertoire without repeats exactly when every record bearing on the form offers it and every other meaning is missing from some bearing record, further records only narrowing its candidates; and, with reports and contents compared decidedly, dated records settle an earlier record's content within a family of histories exactly when every rival giving the same record with another content is told apart by some dated control. Enacted scenes and cooperative naming are instances of the second law, retrospective rereading of its form for records read through a family of explanations, and the four-moment source histories of the third; their failures (naming from one place, mistimed naming, a later route change, an omitted temporary change) lie on the laws' failing side. Observing lies within the downstream: under every law reading into each condition a schedule of listed levels, two balanced courses with the same formations present, the part acting alike and the rest following the law, apart at the start only in a stock, stand alike at each step at every condition not downstream of the stock by the readings formed by then (`apart_only_downstream`). So a formation's exchange responds to the stock's difference only through a level it reads that lies downstream of the stock (`exchange_alike_off_downstream`), direct observation being the route of length zero. Where runs part, neither the prefix nor the law fixes the way: two runs part at a step where their courses through the map are alike before it and differ at it; the prefixes before it are one, so no extrapolation, whatever it is given, the law included, is right on both (`Parting.not_right_on_both`). With moves and configurations listed and steps decided, the extrapolation built from the process is right at a step on every run exactly where no two runs part there (`Parting.world_right_iff`). Where no two runs from a configuration part, with the values decided and the run held by a list of N configurations, the extrapolation by the least bound, given nothing, is right from 2N − 1 on (`Parting.leastBound_right`), as for determined laws, which part nowhere (`Parting.unparted_of_determined`). | 取得25项定理／52项审计；构造37／65；传递27／45；分节23／51；绑定23／41；行动场景16／29；经历选择22／46；回溯绑定20／36；历史绑定10／36（最新304项继承定理／549项审计分列）。空间、类别、目录、准则、初次巡查与合作命名仍有给定；历史组合要求身份、时序、校准和变化／扰动范围，不提供新的资源耗用律；四时刻历史是对每类历史成立之律的实例。经验：36人1,508次命名，三秒467次其他注视而零所指，另45次两者零，五秒前类393次；75人451试次主动−配对−1.34个百分点，95%区间[−10.38,+7.69]不证等效。五篇原典约束自动迁移，未新复算：下一重点是在匹配程序中区分访问取得、来源／次序保持与实际使用，分别测当前依赖及旧内容认可。任务差异的原因、心理实现、演化及意识未完成。 新增学习方式传递30项定理／50项审计，独立重建157项继承定理／251项审计。精确实数复制、两种方式、任务回报转换、有限供给及构造明示给定，校准重新取得；转化、擦除和材料限制保留失败。相对增殖及局部成功不定义生成性，不证明无条件长程对齐或全部认知完成。 观察（`Becoming.Observation`，记录 observation-is-a-route）：无公理，不判定条件，也不需条件有限；律所读的时程是给定的；在下游者是否不同、带误差的量度、律读哪个时程都不推出。读法减弱为 `apart_only_downstream` 所表明的，因为观察不是依赖之外的第二种关系这一说法超出该定理（D43）；观察能否由依赖推出，已在交换之步解决：条件的相等可判定、活动需要支持时，单凭依赖恰好带出随某水平上升至一界的回应，有总量固定的补时带出每个回应，所以观察只对没有补的下降回应才真是第二种关系（what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level）；在二者都是耦合、观察依水平而依赖依阈值的意义上，它不是第二种关系，这是 D45 采用的读法（where-a-level-couples）；二者都不在这一环节之内。分开（`Becoming.Parting`，记录 identification-where-runs-part）：无公理；逆命题需要列出的移动与配置、已定的步与已定的值；运行分开之处，配置之界不界定任何历程（`Parting.free_unbounded`）；无穷或不可判定的过程中依过程所建之外推不推出。两个环节不加前提，不改定理（D43）；它们在本条目中的位置是 AI 的提议，未给论证。<br>Acquisition 25 theorems/52 audits; formation 37/65; transmission 27/45; grouping 23/51; binding 23/41; enacted scenes 16/29; encounter selection 22/46; retrospective binding 20/36; historical binding 10/36 (latest 304 inherited theorems/549 audits separate). Spatial/category laws, catalogue, criterion, initial surveys and cooperative naming retain supplied premises; historical composition requires identity, chronology, calibration and change/disturbance envelopes, with no new law of resource use; the four-moment histories are instances of a law for every type of histories. Empirical: 36 infants/1,508 events, 467 three-second other-without-target gaze cases plus 45 zero on both; 393 former cases at five seconds. For 75 participants/451 trials, Curiosity−Yoked −1.34 percentage points, 95% interval [−10.38,+7.69], not equivalence. Five primary papers constrain automatic transfer, without new reanalysis. Next distinguish access acquisition, source/order retention and actual use in a matched procedure, measuring present reliance and old-content endorsement separately. Causes of task differences, psychological realization, evolution and consciousness remain incomplete. Adds 30 new theorems / 50 audits for procedure transmission, independently rebuilt with 157 inherited theorems / 251 audits. Exact-real copying, two procedures, task-return conversion, finite supply and construction remain supplied; calibration is reacquired. Conversion/erasure and limited material preserve failures. Neither relative-frequency increase nor local task success defines generativity; no unconditional long-run alignment or full cognition completion. Observation (`Becoming.Observation`, record observation-is-a-route): no axiom, no decision on conditions and no finiteness of conditions; the schedule a law reads is given; that what lies downstream differs, measurement with error and which schedule a law reads are not shown. The reading is weakened to what `apart_only_downstream` shows, since the claim that observation is no second relation beside dependence goes beyond the theorem (D43); whether observation could be derived from dependence is settled at the step of the exchange: with equality of conditions decided and activity needing support, dependence alone carries of a level exactly the responses rising with it up to a bound, and with a complement at a fixed total every response, so observation is genuinely second only for a falling response with no complement (what-dependence-alone-carries-of-a-level); in the sense that both are coupling, observation by level and dependence by threshold, it is no second relation, a reading adopted in D45 (where-a-level-couples); neither is part of this link. Parting (`Becoming.Parting`, record identification-where-runs-part): no axiom; the converse needs listed moves and configurations, decided steps and decided values; where runs part, a bound on configurations bounds no course (`Parting.free_unbounded`), and the extrapolation built from the process is not shown for infinite or undecided processes. Both links add no premise and change no theorem (D43); their place in this entry is an AI proposal, with no argument given. | [复核时的理论与完整目标 / Account and full objective as | F01 | 过程核心、谓词与路径组合<br>Process core predication and composition | 给定变化关系后，有限历史通过实际共同中间配置组合；谓词可陈述保持性、可能性及全称终点性质，忠实抽象有明确局部条件。 生成逻辑首轮在左单位与原生上下文相容条件下，证明资源演算的收缩可容许当且仅当实际复制可达；有限供给有精确付费替代界限。 新的有限逐前缀继续谓词接入同一核心：每个请求步骤可继续，全部完成终点满足目标；有相反回应与成功后须调整的机制见证。 第二轮从原生占用规律推出局部历史允许的固定上下文精确判据；单相关支承位摘要保留所选工作负载的执行与整体事件步数预算。 第三轮证明：当前接入相同，允许改接范围内占用一致，当且仅当全部允许有限历史可行性一致。 第四轮将有来源保证的记录与逐位置核对接到同一原生取得过程；任意有限执行投影合法，并保留取得／使用预算。 无预设元逻辑续研提出同一规则的构成性根据候选：同一根据在同一认可要求下直接承接；完整奠基仍待完成。 接续区分由引入／使用构成的共同指向与经验再辨认；给定有限语法中，无来源、无周围假设的原子型 A → A 项必定返回所给根据。 固定规律下的返回判据现刻画实际历史中全部端点可能性的保留；无返回具有构造性的丧失端点见证，不完整问题可遮蔽它。 代入接续构造嵌套绑定中的替换：全部消费者求值保真，当且仅当各替换项供给原位置的根据；单假设代入及先后组合由此接通。 构成性评估现区分引用的设立、执行、辨认与根据：局部承接无需逐次新增认可命题，但不因此获得全部元推理资源的无前提正当性。 | Given a transition relation, finite histories compose through a shared reached configuration; predicates express invariance, possibility and universal endpoint properties, with explicit local conditions for faithful abstraction. A logic pilot proves contraction admissible in a resource calculus exactly when duplication is reachable, under a left unit and primitive context compatibility; finite provision has a sharp paid-replacement bound. A finite prefix-sensitive obligation extends the same core: every requested step can continue and all completed endpoints meet the target, with mechanisms for opposite responses and adjustment after success. A continuation derives the exact frozen-context criterion from native occupancy laws; a relevant-slot summary preserves selected execution and merged-state event-count budgets. A third round proves that, at equal current routes, agreement on permitted-slot occupancy is equivalent to agreement on every allowed finite-word feasibility judgment. A fourth round connects maintained records and per-slot validation to the same native acquisition process, proving finite-trace projection and separate acquisition/use accounting. Direct metalogical inquiry adds a constitutive identity-rule candidate: re-presenting the same ground under the same assessment requirement; full grounding remains unfinished. A continuation distinguishes constituted binding from independent reidentification: every closed source-free atomic endomorphism in the supplied finite language returns its argument. A fixed-law return criterion now characterizes preservation of all endpoint possibilities along an actual history; absent return has a constructive lost endpoint witness, and incomplete questions can hide it. Constructed substitution under nested binders preserves evaluation for every consumer exactly when each replacement supplies its original position ground; single insertion and successive composition are connected. The constitutive assessment now distinguishes institution, execution, recognition and warrant: local retention needs no fresh endorsement proposition per use, but does not thereby justify every metainferential resource without presuppositions. | 逻辑、配置／事件域及变化关系明确给定，未由此导出全部规律或伦理方向。完整说明附六模块61项空依赖审计，其中核心18项、新教学定理12项；可能性、无执行时的空真与实际进展分别保留。旧回执仍可查。 新38项定理／65项总审计（51空），含顺序复用不保证重叠、上下文干扰与完整补给包不可复制的反例。形成的是即时就绪；任意有限准备已计入可达性。未导出元逻辑或确立前沿新颖性；第一轮提出的上下文与摘要目标已由本次有范围结果接续。 新11项定理继承39项审计；50项中44空、6仅标准依赖。终点正确可遗漏中断；实际调度、物理时间与目标伦理根据另列。 新37项定理／220项闭包审计（124空）；新证明无经典选择。初始完整相容性、局部历史与固定占用条件明示；总量摘要失败，单支承摘要不能认证遗漏位置的相容性。整体预算不等于保持其他占用的准备，步数不等于真实时间。Iris/Leaf已有先例，仍属完成的整合而非新颖性证明。 新24项定理／42项总审计（30空）；原生三步历史产生旧工作负载等价、扩展范围可区分的配置。旧摘要加目的地不足以总能更新摘要。允许范围、原子性和身份给定；不禁止无损编码，未解决新读数取得或确立前沿新颖性。已完成交错研究直接复用。 新28项定理／70项闭包审计（38空）；新证明8空、20仅标准依赖，无经典选择。无关改变可继续；指定n次失效后读取／使用需n+2单位，任意有限预算有耗尽见证。原子可信读取、全部写入版本维护及原子核对／使用给定；此局部连接已完成，现实对应及新颖性未证。 新11项形式审计均为空命名公理报告，仍使用 Lean 元理论。混合认可标准可不反身，反身性不独自保证接续；Curry 型固定点控制限制任意自我担保。实查不代替内容／根据辨认的哲学说明，既有无前提不可能性未证，亦未宣称完成或新逻辑。 新7项定理／21项含继承声明审计均空；继承14项不重计为新。关系保持由语法归纳得到；额外上下文或来源各有替代输入的见证。use解释、语法及元理论仍给定，实际绑定与假设代入的最终根据未证；非全体复合类型或任意程序的唯一性，不宣称新颖性。 新10项 Lean 定理／14项空审计，另重编23项继承定理／28项审计。身份、规律及端点含义给定；返回不抹去历史，未证明物理不可逆、伦理排序或零实质公理完成。仅作者反审。 再增9项定理／19项含继承审计，13空、6仅Quot.sound；同类型误接可改答案，同值可掩盖误接。有限语法、绑定解释、假设保证及实际输入满足公开；求值同一不独自给出完整意义／来源同一，无前提元逻辑仍未完成。 本次完成的是既有候选的哲学评估，零新增定理、未重编；先前笼统的绑定最终根据问题由具体的元推理推广不足取代。三条推广路线与实质对手已评估；严格目标未完成，也未证明不可能。<br>Logic, configuration/event domains and dynamics remain explicit inputs; this does not derive every law or ethical direction. The exposition checks six modules with 61 empty dependency audits, including 18 core audits and 12 new pedagogical theorems; possibility, vacuity and actual progress remain distinct. The earlier receipt is retained. New pilot: 38 theorems / 65 total audits (51 empty), retaining serial/overlap, contextual interference and funded-package duplication boundaries. Immediate readiness forms; unrestricted reachability already includes preparation. Metalogic and novelty are not established; the first-round context/abstraction target is continued by the scoped results below. Eleven new theorems plus 39 inherited audits; 44 of 50 reports empty, six standard only. Correct endpoints can omit interruption; scheduling, physical time and ethical target warrant remain separate. Continuation: 37 new theorems / 220 closure audits (124 empty), no new choice dependency. Full initial compatibility, a valid local history and a frozen frame are explicit. Counts lose readiness; the selected summary loses hidden-slot compatibility. Merged-state budgets do not imply preparation preserving held occupancy, and steps are not physical time. Iris/Leaf precedents remain credited; the scoped integration is complete, without a novelty claim. Reconfiguration: 24 new theorems / 42 total audits (30 empty). A native three-event history witnesses old-workload equivalence and separation under wider access; old active-view plus destination cannot always determine the new view. Permission scope, identities and atomicity remain supplied. Lossless encodings remain possible; observation acquisition and novelty are not established. Completed interleaving work is reused. Acquired-use continuation: 28 new theorems / 70 closure audits (38 empty); new reports are 8 empty and 20 standard only, with no choice. Unrelated change is allowed; n specified invalidation cycles then read/use cost n+2 units, and every finite budget has an exhaustion witness. Coherent atomic reads, full writer revision maintenance and atomic validate/use remain supplied. This local bridge is complete; realization and novelty are not established. Eleven new formal audits have empty named-axiom reports but retain Lean's metatheory. Mixed standards can fail reflexivity; reflexivity alone does not ensure composition. A Curry fixed-point control limits unrestricted self-certification. Formal checks do not establish philosophical recognition of content/ground; neither impossibility, completed presuppositionless grounding nor a new logic is claimed. Seven new theorems / 21 total declaration audits are empty, including 14 inherited audits. Relation preservation follows by syntax induction; ambient and introduced grounds give controls. Context-use interpretation, syntax and metatheory remain supplied; actual binding and hypothetical-substitution warrant remain open. The uniqueness scope is atomic terms, not all compound types or arbitrary programs; no novelty claim. Ten new Lean theorems / 14 empty audits; 23 inherited theorems / 28 audits separately rebuilt. Identity, law and endpoint meanings remain supplied. Returning does not erase history; no physical irreversibility, ethical ranking or zero-substantive-axiom completion is claimed. Author counterreview only. Nine further theorems / 19 total audits: 13 empty, 6 only Quot.sound. Same-type capture can change answers; coincident values can conceal it. Finite grammar, binding interpretation, hypothetical guarantees and satisfied input criteria remain explicit. Denotational agreement alone does not identify full content or provenance; presuppositionless metalogic remains unfinished. This continuation completes the philosophical assessment of the existing candidate, with zero new theorems or recompilation. The vague ultimate-binding gap is replaced by a specific insufficiency in promotion to metainferential warrant. Three routes and substantive rivals are assessed; the strict target remains unmet, not proved impossible. | [核心源码 / Core | F20 | 尺度近似与条件传递的形式检查<br>Formal checks of scale approximation and condition transport | 有限时域误差递推、两尺度组合、指标校准和条件传递经过实查，并保留耦合变化、误差累积及总量丢失局部条件的反例。后续检查实际证据取得、描述修订、误差余量与回应，保留耗时、读数偏差、工作耗尽及支持不足的失败和固定回应成功。同一时限下的查询／直接回应比较进一步以共同可行回应的交集识别有关区别，保留查询过晚与支持不足，并修订选择条件。变化依存下还须校准记录有效期；刷新后仍可能失效，后续变化界给出有条件的修复。 此前保留旧读数的留出检验保留最大变化界被突破及迁移失败；该轮所试区间均宽于控制器软目标带，完整经验认证尚未成立。 后续局部历史模型改善18项留出预测比较，胜过同容量跨通道模型；三个窄百分位区间仍有实际失败。 接续充分性进一步要求活动后的每个观察类仍支持剩余时域的回应；一般递推等价于依记录分支的有限方案，保留眼前成功而后继冲突，以及取得区别／实际改变依存两种修复。 共同接续进一步证明：两份依各自记录分支的有限方案，在共同作用的局部投影覆盖与递推共同可实施条件下合成；共享消耗可使各自成功而共同后继阻塞。 有根据的相互接续从初始共同支持、一步共同可实施与相互保持推导递推方案相容；保留空许诺、空真保持、无启动能力及相位错开。 证据经取得到使用之间的变化传播后，须同时支持共同启动及两份局部方案的适用范围；已接回原合成定理，保留真实旧读数失效、原子保留修复及未受管理损失。 | Checked finite-horizon error propagation, two-scale composition, readout calibration and condition transport retain counterexamples for changed coupling, accumulated error and local conditions lost by exact totals. Changed dependencies require calibrated temporal validity; a fresh reading can still fail, and bounded subsequent change supports a conditional repair. A subsequent local-history model improves 18 held-out comparisons and beats an equal-size coupled model; three narrow percentile intervals retain actual misses. Continuation adequacy further requires each post-activity observation class to support the remaining horizon; the recursion is equivalent to a finite observation-dependent plan, retaining later conflict and repairs through information or actual dependency change. Joint continuation composes two finite local observation-dependent plans under faithful projection of joint effects and recursive joint executability; shared consumption can block joint successors despite separate success. Seeded mutual continuation derives recursive plan compatibility from initial joint support, one-step joint availability and mutual preservation, retaining empty promises, vacuous preservation, absent ignition capacity and phase mismatch. Evidence propagated through acquisition-to-use changes must certify both the shared seed and local-plan domains; the existing composition theorem is reused, with stale truthful records, atomic-reservation repair and unmediated loss retained. | | F21 | 组织、选择与尺度对应的形式检查<br>Formal checks of organization, choice and scale correspondence | 所选行动影响之差进入有限时域误差界；阈值余量足以保持指定选择，近阈值反例及共享支持被他者消耗后的计划失败均经过实查。 新构造证明实际描述组合、保持历史与区域差异的必要性，并接通有支持的选择与后续接触。 | Differences in selected action effects enter the finite-horizon error bound; threshold margins suffice to preserve specified choices, with checked near-threshold counterexamples and failed retained plans after another participant consumes shared support. The new construction proves actual view composition and the consequential role of retained history and regional differences, connecting supported selection to subsequent contact. | 旧14项定理／63项继承审计及新26项定理／77项继承审计分别保留。标准逻辑，无自定义公理；规律、路线、保持、费用、时序与对应明确给定。后续经验校准、分子证据及有限生态回返不增加定理，不从拟合残差推出普遍误差界，也不完成整个生物闭环。<br>Earlier 14 theorems / 63 inherited audits and new 26 theorems / 77 inherited audits remain separate. | F22 | 关系恢复、组合唯一性与接口重建<br>Relation recovery, composition uniqueness and reconstructed interfaces | 继承校准、结构对手、组合唯一性及实际展开的接合／替换、分支继续与同规律错误反例；另增空历史探针、局部单位唯一性与关系同构下保持、完整接续充要条件和整个状态接口恢复。容量及内部序列丢失反例保留。 最新从原有机制直接证明摘要充分性，推出有效规律与全部有限历史精确对应，不另给Exact或同态前提。 普遍化检验给出任意三元关系恢复的充要条件；完整成功答案与最少状态／边仍允许不同构机制，确定且可达、后续可区分时恢复唯一对应。 后续以共同条件下的复合接续问题推出有限分支双模拟，并由准确约简答案构造可从每个相容原生起点提升的编码规律；结构唯一性不再要求确定性。 再以后继紧致性放宽有限分支，取得结构对应及完整无限编码路径提升；有无限多个可区分后继的正例，也有全部有限复合答案相同而无限持续不同的反例。 共同问题再在明示成对紧致性下迫出步骤／接合对应并提升有限逐节点方案；实际占用／释放推出共享冲突。全部孤立共同问题仍可能遗漏指定部分的共享身份，固定搭配问题在有限实例中恢复。 再以完整下层支持覆盖及同级独占支持分离，将任意有限方案接到全局无重复与一次共同占用历史；遗漏关系的失败及修复正例均检查。每个固定 n 阶成功清单仍可漏掉 n+1 阶共享容量限制。 变化中的形成、撤去、占用与释放具有精确证书保持条件；任意允许有限历史在同一原生规律内都有完成后缀。静态清单会遗漏实际占用，释放／形成可恢复或建立准备；无限允许调度仍可能永不推进目标。 全体有限一阶身份、生成和接合真值一致，甚至有一个保留任意有限参数公式的映射，仍不足以迫出全范围可达结构的原生同构；Cantor 对角线给出真实不同构对手。保真映射再有全局覆盖则恢复同构。 纠正：原反例未对准共同必要生成基础，不能作为原目标停止证据；给定位置之间可达未解释其构成性形成。 纠正后由同一部分接合关系形成全部有限情境，准确说明必须保留改变接续可能性的区别，冗余说明亦有共同因子；固定片段接合结果唯一时，共同内容带有唯一对应的操作。实际历史的全部嵌套接合问题恰好保留开始／结束接口，不抹去不同内部事件。 后续允许接合本身有多个结果：在排中律下，有限结果纤维及更丰富的分支问答迫出共同接合关系，原指定输入能实现每个代码结果；描述的共同核是接合的互模拟时，二者无需前提即成立；检验伙伴随同一编码转换，任意有限接合树提升。共同表示仍可有两个可区分结果。 不共用片段载体时，经关联角色相似的根无需有限或判定即可导出单位置接合的共同细化及与既有投影相容的唯一保接合因子；在排中律及局部有限下，关联同测的根亦然；但无引用关联问题会使所有有局部单位的片段同测，遗漏已消耗历史的不可自接合。保留同一配置的两个引用位置后，跨载体的有限形成历史可以匹配，精确说明必区分同一过程的空历史与已消耗历史。 有限宏步骤由同一过程路径形成，完整宏问答可精确翻译；在排中律下，接收侧有限分支及翻译问答一致迫出不同原生步数的有限历史匹配。全部非空前缀问答仍可遗漏中途失续；在实际到达位置开放后缀检验可恢复区别。 有限条件程序进一步按已到达条件分支或提前结束；宏问答恢复之外，相同语法与相似的起点可匹配每个部分历史并保留余项；在排中律及接收侧有限分支下，全部原生问答同测亦然。完整执行恰好等于展开到结束；同起点同原生位置的余项差异迫使精确说明区分。插入活动可使待用接续失效，实际前中后段仍接为一条历史。 | Retains calibration, structural rivals, compositional uniqueness and native unfolding’s joining/substitution, branch-continuation distinctions and same-law error cases. Adds empty-history probes, unique units and transport, an exact completion criterion and state-interface recovery, retaining capacity and internal-word-loss counters. The latest concrete summary-sufficiency proof derives an effective law and exact finite-history correspondence from the unchanged mechanism, without a separate Exact or homomorphism premise. Successor compactness further replaces finite branching, preserving structural correspondence and lifting whole infinite code runs; a genuinely infinitely branching positive model contrasts with equal finite-modal answers but different infinite realization. Joint questions further force step/joint correspondence under explicit correlated-pair compactness and lift finite nodewise plans; acquisition/release derives shared-support conflict. Complete isolated joint profiles can still lose designated-part aliasing, recovered by fixed-partner questions in the finite witness. Complete descendant support and sibling exclusion further connect arbitrary finite plans to distinct global requests and a common holding history; omission failures and a positive repair are checked. Every fixed n-ary success list can still miss an (n+1)-ary capacity restriction. All finite first-order identity, step and joint truths, even preserved by one coherent elementary map on every finite source tuple, do not force native isomorphism of wholly root-reachable worlds. Cantor gives a genuine obstruction; adding global coverage yields isomorphism. Corrected: the counterexample is not accepted as a stopping test for a common necessary generative basis: reachability between supplied positions does not explain their constitution. After correction, all finite contexts arise from the same partial joining law. Exact accounts must retain differences affecting continuation and have a common factor even when redundant. All nested joining questions on admitted histories recover exactly their beginning/ending interface while internal event differences remain real. The continuation permits multivalued joining itself: under excluded middle, finite outcome fibers and richer branch questions force a common joining relation, and each code result lifts from the originally specified inputs; where the descriptions' one kernel is a bisimulation of the joining, both hold with no premise. Partners in questions translate through the same encoding, and every finite joining tree lifts. The common account can retain two distinguishable outcomes. Across different fragment carriers, roots alike through their incidence roles yield, with no finiteness or decision, a common refinement with one-position lifting and a unique join-preserving factor commuting with given projections; roots agreeing on every incidence question do so under excluded middle with local finiteness. Unanchored incidence questions nevertheless collapse all local-unit fragments, losing the spent history’s impossible self-join. Retaining two reference slots in the same configuration recovers matched finite formation histories; exact accounts must distinguish idle and spent histories within one process. Finite blocks arise from paths of the same process and all block questions compile exactly. Under excluded middle, receiving-side finite branching and translated-answer agreement match histories with different native lengths. All nonempty-prefix questions can still omit intermediate loss; suffix tests at the actual reached position restore the distinction. Finite guarded recipes branch or end at reached conditions. | F29 | 纠正条件与组合实查<br>Correction conditions and checked composition | 精确接受分解、维护界、旧价值反例、Bellman 组合、观察一致性及通道交接均经 Lean 检查。 新增完整有限权重反例、观察限制、可执行修复及近似界。 新增Bayes一致观察变化、主动取得信息的最优界、风险分离失效及纠正传递。 新增记录探测、版本失效、重复交接不变量及四类失败见证。 新增观察类共同动作充要条件、记录证书限制、随机界及测试—使用保留区间。 新增互补观察、任意复制失败、独立／共同误差、父汇总干预与联合负载检验。 新增全情境可行性等价、关系组合、任意有限环路及复合探测、成对修订保持与可观察实现分离。 进一步区分完整端点关系与实际请求历史：逐段准备新路径，同时保护每个在途请求所引用的旧路径。 局部机制可以在其范围内保证某一效果，而这一范围未必在此后每次变化中都持续，所以所研究的是认知与处境变化之中的边界条件和方向；主词是因应转化（AI 所取的工作名），因为它不预设分开的纠正者与被纠正者；可纠正性保留为AI对齐读者桥接。 | Lean checks acceptance factorization, maintenance boundaries, stale-value failure, Bellman composition, observation uniformity and channel handoff. Adds a complete finite-weight counterexample, observation restriction, executable repair and approximation bounds. Adds Bayesian-consistent observation change, optimal sensing bounds, risk-floor failure and correction transport. Adds explicit probing, record invalidation, repeated-handoff invariants and four failure controls. Adds the observation-class common-action equivalence, transcript-certificate limit, randomized bounds and reserved test/use interval. Adds complementary observation, arbitrary duplication failure, independent/common error laws, parent-summary interventions and joint-load tests. Adds all-context feasibility equivalence, relational composition, arbitrary finite cycles and aggregate probes, paired-revision preservation and separation from observable realization. Complete endpoint relations are further distinguished from actual request histories: prepare a new route incrementally while protecting each in-flight request’s referenced route. A local mechanism may guarantee an effect within its range without that range persisting through every later change, so the question studied is conditions and direction through changing understanding and circumstances; the primary term is responsive transformation (an AI working name), since it presupposes no separate corrector and corrected object; corrigibility remains an AI-alignment reader bridge. | 原34项定理／56项审计保留，新增31项定理／54项审计；Lean 4.30.0-rc2与固定Mathlib，标准逻辑依赖公开。论文对应单独核读，不由内核自动证明；未宣称普遍可纠正性。 本轮新增28项定理／40项审计，31/54继承分列；再新增23项定理／45项审计，原子双通道重复协议已完成；无条件活性、非原子实现和真实对应未证。 再新增34项定理／58项审计，两个模块无继承仓库证明；标准choice依赖公开。普通互斥为成功对照，未证完整双通道并发细化或实际AI停机。 本次37项新定理／58项新审计，含继承共89项；101及10,201个精确概率网格通过。未把条件独立和实际系统边界藏入数量定义。 本次再增39项定理／55项审计：38项空依赖、17项仅propext，无继承仓库证明；256对关系、4,096组三元组合、510个环路根值及1,284种成对修订通过。不是经验AI验证。 版本接续新增33项定理／62项审计，继承62项定理／100项审计分列；4,512个状态、63,168条转移、100次交接及80次受控线程试验通过。保留原子取得与 pin 登记、生命周期及有限资源前提；既有网络更新获归属，实际AI对应与独立原创性未证。 本次为解释修订，无新数学结果；既有条件证明、处境变化反例及仍有根据的责任保留，不把无条件保证设为方向完成门槛。<br>Original 34 theorems / 56 audits retained; 31 theorems / 54 audits added with Lean 4.30.0-rc2 and pinned Mathlib. Standard dependencies disclosed. External theorem correspondence is separately audited, not kernel-certified; no universal corrigibility claim. This round adds 28 theorems / 40 audits, with inherited 31/54 separate; a further 23 theorems / 45 audits complete an atomic two-channel repeated protocol. Unconditional liveness, non-atomic implementation and real correspondence remain unproved. A further 34 theorems / 58 audits in two modules with no inherited repository proofs; standard choice dependency disclosed. Ordinary mutexes are the successful control; full concurrent refinement of the two-channel model and actual AI shutdown are unproved. This round: 37 new theorems / 58 new audits, 89 including inheritance; 101 and 10,201 exact probability grid points pass. Independence and actual system boundaries are explicit obligations, not consequences of counting. Adds 39 theorems / 55 audits: 38 empty and 17 using only propext, with no inherited repository proofs. Checks cover 256 relation pairs, 4,096 composition triples, 510 cycle/root cases and 1,284 paired revisions. No empirical AI validation. Versioned continuation adds 33 theorems / 62 audits, with inherited 62 theorems / 100 audits separate; 4,512 states, 63,168 transitions, 100 handoffs and 80 controlled thread trials pass. Atomic acquisition with pin registration, lifetime and finite-resource premises remain; established network updates retain credit, while actual AI correspondence and independent novelty remain open. This is an interpretive revision with no new mathematical result. Existing conditional proofs, changed-circumstance failures and warranted responsibilities remain; unconditional assurance is not the direction’s completion gate. | [Lean 源码 / Lean Subsequent inspection forms unlisted correction addresses, separates local success from scoped exhaustion, and rechecks current configuration and authority. A further continuation applies correlated fault envelopes separately to observation and delegated permission, deriving actual routes, mandate fidelity, paid execution and replay rejection. Causal formulas now compute reviewer fault envelopes, supporting atomic organizational reform that retains the mandate, renews authority labels and reserves correction capacity. Retained causal descriptions now form a common certificate envelope; relevant observation refines it into actual correction while some distinctions remain, or current sufficient evidence permits immediate action. Structural answers and witness failures are now interpreted jointly; compatible candidates remain, no designated infallible witness is required, and explicit distinguishability supports actual correction. Paired control/fault possibilities now expand through admitted changes and narrow through sequential reports, connecting current coverage across lawful finite histories to actual correction. A bounded interpreter now derives change scope from executed writes, transports actual and candidate configurations through the same semantics, and couples permissions, generation, recording and old-command revocation in one abstract commit. Sequential overwrites and a durable pending description now cover finite interruption histories; a supported uninterrupted suffix publishes, while evidence coherence does not ensure ordinary shutdown remains available. The stopping connection during recovery is now complete: with a protected current grant/channel and three-step reserve and bounded interruptions, every sufficiently long supervisor sequence completes shutdown while retaining the incomplete description. Both are instances of general laws for every partial law: a property holds after every admitted course exactly when every admitted step keeps it; counted work finishes in work + 1 steps exactly when the fuel covers them; and where every admitted step either takes a kept exit or lowers a margin by one, every admitted course longer than the margin ends at the exit. | 四项条件性理论成果完成；59项新Lean定理／102项声明审计和14,295项有限记录匹配。42项空依赖，其余仅标准公理。保留遗漏模型、查询耗尽、权限失效、部件不足、失真交接及迁移资金不足。常规有限规划匹配；真实部署、任意发现及普遍收敛未证。作者反审，不冒充独立验收。 接续另有24项新定理／45项审计、25,284项匹配记录；21项空依赖，其余仅标准公理。有限忠实界面、检查成本和原子执行明确；权限俘获不由凭据相等解决。 部分失陷接续另有37项新定理／68项审计、85,680项匹配记录（29项空依赖）。可用性要求任意两个纳入故障集合不覆盖全部审查者；真实故障覆盖、受保护执行边界与委托正当性仍须根据。 最终执行器直接检查受保护委托范围；原仅凭认证的失陷失败保留。 组织改革另增28项定理／48项审计、33,648项匹配记录（28项声明空依赖）；共同控制、重复支承、遗漏原因及预算不足反例保留。因果描述与根原因覆盖、改革授权、受保护的原子实现仍给定；非分布式迁移或伦理正当性证明。 证据接续另增24项定理／47项审计、19,808项匹配记录（23项声明空依赖）。逐候选可用不推出共同可用；误导记录、未记载变化及查询耗尽保留。候选／原因覆盖、诊断忠实、稳定查询使用及权限仍明示，已完成的一般模型修订不重计。 可失实检查另增24项定理／41项审计、18,684项匹配记录（22项空依赖）。完整记录的相反答案可辨认性等价于跨候选任意两失陷集合不覆盖全体；缺报须有实际收到的共同幸存见证。循环自证、重复支承、耗尽及超范围共同失陷保留；真实覆盖、忠实幸存报告、稳定记录执行及权限仍给定。 时间接续另增34项定理／66项审计、35,486项匹配记录（32项空依赖）。每时刻失陷上限不等于整段收集上限；移动失陷可造成全假记录。复查后的真实停止、后续受阻保留先前消耗及变化范围低报失败均实查。真实转移／报告规律、受保护记录和最终命令区间仍给定；未实现物理恢复或分布式迁移。 受控执行接续另增27项定理／57项审计、24,416项匹配记录（15项空依赖）。准确的已知变换可省去重查，具体19单位停止、18单位不足；权限拒绝与重放均实查。额外写入通道可使记录完全不变而实际覆盖失真。真实语义、完整控制入口、报告／失陷范围与原子执行仍需根据；暴露状态写入不等于物理恢复，未实现受保护运行时。 中断恢复新增22项定理／48项审计、27,888项有限匹配（17项空依赖）。剩余 n 项写入及发布需要 n+1单位与对应时距；重启保留消耗。九单位时恢复耗尽、活动仍运行且普通停止受阻；待恢复标记丢失可暴露失实认证。持久原语、原子微步骤／发布、覆盖写入、无旁路和当前报告对应仍供应。旧指令成果迁移为 mediated-evidence-transport，恢复增量为 recoverable-mediated-change，不重算既有27项。 保留停止增量新增21项定理／51项审计（18空）、7,272项有限匹配；复用恢复相容性及旧三步命令，从剩余 R 单位在至多 R−2个监督周期停止，无需恢复成功。独立停止授权／通道、持久保护、有限延迟、尝试成本及监督继续执行仍供应；超界中断保留运行失败。旧九单位反例属旧协议，新入口拒绝而非追溯修复。canonical ID: reserved-shutdown-during-recovery。<br>Four scoped theoretical deliverables complete; 59 new Lean theorems / 102 declaration audits and 14,295 matched finite records. Forty-two empty dependencies; others use standard axioms only. Omitted models, inquiry depletion, authority failure, missing parts, corrupt transfer and migration underfunding remain. Conventional finite planning matches; deployment, arbitrary discovery and universal convergence are unestablished. Author counterreview, not independent acceptance. The continuation adds 24 theorems / 45 audits and 25,284 matched records; 21 empty dependencies, otherwise standard axioms. Finite faithful inspection, charged reads and atomic dispatch remain explicit; credential equality does not resolve authority capture. Partial-compromise work adds 37 theorems / 68 audits and 85,680 matched records (29 empty dependencies). Availability requires that no two admitted fault sets cover all reviewers; actual fault coverage, the protected boundary and mandate legitimacy still require warrant. The final executor directly checks the protected scope; the earlier attestation-only failure is retained. Reconfiguration adds 28 theorems / 48 audits and 33,648 matched records (28 declarations with empty dependencies). Shared control, duplicate support, omitted causes and inadequate budgets remain. Causal formulas/root coverage, reform approval and protected atomic realization are supplied; distributed migration and ethical legitimacy are not proved. Evidence continuation adds 24 theorems / 47 audits and 19,808 matched records (23 declarations with empty dependencies). Per-model availability need not survive pooling; misleading records, unobserved change and inquiry depletion remain. Candidate/root coverage, diagnostic fidelity, stable query/use and authority stay explicit; completed general model revision is not recounted. Fallible inspection adds 24 theorems / 41 audits and 18,684 matched records (22 empty dependencies). Opposite-answer complete-transcript diagnosability is equivalent to cross-candidate pairwise fault noncoverage; partial transcripts require received common-survivor evidence. Self-certification, duplicate support, exhaustion and uncovered joint faults remain; actual coverage, faithful surviving reports, stable recording/execution and authority remain supplied. Temporal continuation adds 34 theorems / 66 audits and 35,486 matched records (32 empty dependencies). Instantaneous fault bounds do not bound the collection window; moving corruption can produce all-false reports. Reinspection-to-shutdown, preservation of spent prefixes and understated-transition failure are checked. Actual transition/report laws, protected recording and the final command interval remain supplied; physical recovery and distributed migration are not implemented. Mediated-control continuation adds 27 theorems / 57 audits and 24,416 matched records (15 empty dependencies). Exact known transforms can avoid reinspection; the worked shutdown costs 19 units and fails with 18. Denied rights and replay are checked. An extra writer can preserve all public records while breaking actual coverage. Physical semantics, complete mediation, report/fault coverage and atomic execution remain conditions; the exposure setter is not physical recovery and no protected runtime is implemented. Interrupted recovery adds 22 theorems / 48 audits and 27,888 finite matches (17 empty dependencies). Remaining n writes plus publication require n+1 units and corresponding time; restart retains consumption. At nine units, recovery exhausts while activity keeps running and ordinary shutdown is blocked; losing the pending marker can expose a false certificate. Durable primitives, atomic microsteps/publication, overwrite semantics, no bypass and current reports remain supplied. The previous instruction result is migrated as mediated-evidence-transport; the delta is recoverable-mediated-change, without recounting the prior 27. Reserved stopping adds 21 theorems / 51 audits (18 empty) and 7,272 finite matches. It reuses recovery coherence and the existing three-step command, stopping within R−2 supervisor cycles from R remaining units without recovery success. Independent stop authority/channel, durable protection, bounded delay, attempt cost and continued scheduling remain supplied; excessive interruption retains a running failure. The old nine-unit failure remains for the old protocol; new admission refuses it rather than repairing it retroactively.
