| E04 | 规范根据的正面论证与实质反审<br>Affirmative grounding arguments and substantive counterreview | 同一完整关系须接受正面解释、保留其事实的强对手和证据评估；思想案例与形式反例各有范围。 | The same complete relationship faces an affirmative explanation, a substantive rival preserving its facts, and evidence assessment; thought cases and formal counterexamples retain their scopes. | 研究评审与来源入口；不把评审次数或原文引用数当作证明。<br>Research review and source entry points; neither review counts nor citation counts constitute proof. | [优先研究整合 / Priority integration](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/NORMATIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md)<br>[原始文献评估 / Primary-source assessment](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/NORMATIVE-WARRANT-SOURCES.md)<br>[复核时的实质反审 / Substantive review as reviewed](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/ZERO-AXIOM-CLASSIFICATION-REVIEW.md)<br>[Sen 阅读与局限 / Sen reading and limits](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/ZERO-AXIOM-CLASSIFICATION-SOURCES.md) |
| E05 | 良知的经典与发展证据<br>Classical and developmental evidence for Liangzhi | 经典文本、社会形成与发展研究为具体机制和解释提供比较；形成动机的证据不直接证明伦理有效性。 | Classical texts and studies of social formation and development constrain particular mechanisms and interpretations; evidence for motivation formation does not directly establish ethical validity. | 各来源按原阅读和研究范围使用；不是新经验实验。<br>Each source retains its recorded reading and evidential scope; no new empirical experiment. | [演化与发展来源 / Evolution and development sources](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION-SOURCES.md)<br>[王阳明原典范围 / Wang Yangming source scope](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/WANG-YANGMING-COMPLETION-SOURCES.md)<br>[实践整合 / Practical integration](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIANGZHI-PRACTICAL-INTEGRATION.md) |
| E06 | 实际对应与证据范围<br>Real-world correspondence and evidential scope | 模型、合成检验、实际观察与哲学根据承担不同工作，保留来源谱系及反例。生命证据连接维护、功能恢复、有效组织及后续参与；区分可投入能力、推断参与、替代损失的生产和净增长。转变研究区分材料保留与变化后的要求；中断形成的谱系实验和生长中单个区室的对照进一步检验既有供给何时不足，支持三类机制的整合。 | Models, synthetic checks, observation and philosophical warrant do different work, preserving provenance and counterexamples. Living evidence connects maintenance, functional recovery, effective organization and later participation, distinguishing recruitable capacity, inferred engagement, replacement production and net growth. Transition studies separate material retention from changed requirements; interrupted-formation lineages and a growing-compartment contrast further test inherited insufficiency, supporting the three-mode synthesis. | 一般生命说明的证据整合已获审查，不等于全部现实均已验证。量恢复不证明全部功能；受损制备可保留残余活性。不同物种、版本和制备不合并成同一历史；分裂停止不等于死亡，荧光不等于精确有效副本数。计数增加不等于功能充分，当前供给不等于历次出生数。批次无物理时长，平均速率不自动成为逐历史上界；普遍更新期限与伦理权威均未证明。<br>The evidence synthesis for the general living account is reviewed, without verifying the entire real domain. Amount restoration does not prove full function; impaired preparations can retain residual activity. Different organisms, versions and preparations are not one history; division arrest is not death, and fluorescence is not an exact functional-copy count. Count increase is not functional sufficiency, and current provision is not cumulative births. Batches supply no physical duration; mean rates do not automatically bound every history. Universal renewal deadlines and ethical authority remain unproved. | [经验范围 / Empirical scope](../history/EMPIRICAL-SCOPE.md)<br>[来源图 / Source map](../history/SOURCE-MAP.md)<br>[成果登记 / Results register](../../../RESULTS.md)<br>[生命机制与原始研究 / Living mechanisms and primary studies](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIVING-DEPENDENCY-MECHANISMS.md)<br>[恢复证据与对应范围 / Recovery evidence and correspondence](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIVING-RECOVERY-EVIDENCE.md)<br>[保留组织与实际功能证据 / Retained organization and functional evidence](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIVING-ORGANIZATION-EVIDENCE.md)<br>[部分能力、参与与周转证据 / Partial capacity, engagement and turnover evidence](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIVING-CAPACITY-EVIDENCE.md)<br>[生命转变原始证据评估 / Living-transition primary-evidence assessment](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIVING-TRANSITION-EVIDENCE.md)<br>[转变评审与既有证明对应 / Transition review and existing-proof correspondence](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIVING-TRANSITION-REVIEW.md)<br>[完整目标验收及未证范围 / Full-goal completion audit and open claims](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIVING-DEPENDENCY-INTEGRATION.md#12-full-goal-completion-audit)<br>[扩展、形成中断与不足证据 / Expansion, interrupted formation and insufficiency evidence](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIVING-EXPANSION-EVIDENCE.md)<br>[独立评审及完整说明验收 / Independent review and general-account acceptance](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/LIVING-EXPANSION-REVIEW.md) |

## 持续维护 / Ongoing maintenance

这份表在原位置持续更新。关联研究发生实质变化时，同时修订中英文论断、状态与链接。未发现相关变化时不改写内容，也不重复运行已通过的证明。

Maintain this same document in place. When linked research changes substantively, revise both languages, status and links together. Without relevant changes, leave the content and successful proof runs untouched.

生成文件的编辑来源是 [双语数据](data/generativity-chain.json)。源文件摘要只用于发现变化，不能代替重读与判断；维护流程和更新记录见 [维护记录 / Maintenance record](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVITY-CHAIN-INDEX-RECORD.md)。

The [bilingual data](data/generativity-chain.json) is the editable source. Source hashes detect changes; they do not replace reading or judgment. Follow the linked maintenance record for the maintenance workflow and update history.
